local callbacks = {}

local function packResults(...)
    return { {...}, select("#", ...) }
end
local unpackValues = table.unpack or unpack
local function unpackResults(values)
    return unpackValues(values[1], 1, values[2])
end
local function normalizeIterator(iterator, state, key)
    if type(iterator) ~= "function" then
        local mt = getmetatable(iterator)
        if mt and mt.__iter then
            return mt.__iter(iterator)
        elseif (not mt or not mt.__call) and type(iterator) == "table" then
            return pairs(iterator)
        end
    end
    return iterator, state, key
end


callbacks.effectLightning = function(valueRef, valueRef2, valueRef3, valueRef4)
    return function(r, m)
        local _, e, f, h, i, k, l, n, o, p
        r = {value = r}
        valueRef3.value.strip(r.value)
        p = valueRef3.value.parts(r.value)
        p = {value = p}
        k = {value = m.Position}
        h = Instance.new("PointLight", m)
        h = {value = h}
        h.value.Color = Color3.fromRGB(200, 150, 255)
        h.value.Range = 55
        h.value.Brightness = 0
        _, i = ipairs, {}
        i[3], i[2], i[1] = 0.22, 0.09, 0
        _, l, f = _(i)
        _, l, f = normalizeIterator(_, l, f)
        i, n = _(l, f)
        f = i
        if (i ~= nil) then
            while true do
                o, e = task.delay, callbacks.runLightningPulse(p, k, valueRef2, h, valueRef3, valueRef)
                o(n, e)
                i, n = _(l, f)
                f = i
                if (i == nil) then
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        valueRef3.value.ring(k.value, Color3.fromRGB(160, 100, 255), 28, 0.4)
        valueRef4.value(k.value, "rbxasset://sounds/impact_explosion_03.mp3", 3, 1.3)
        valueRef3.value.shards(k.value, Color3.fromRGB(220, 180, 255), Enum.Material.Neon, 16, 28, 0.6)
        f, _ = callbacks.invokeFade(valueRef3, r), task.delay
        _(2.8, f)
        return
    end
end

callbacks.applyUsernameAppearance = function(valueRef, valueRef2, valueRef3, valueRef4, valueRef5, contextRef, valueRef6)
    return function(r, m)
        local _, b, e, f, g, h, k, l, n, o, p, s
        p = valueRef5.value
        if (not valueRef5.value) then
            p = (not r)
        end
        if (not p) then
            p = (r == "")
        end
        if p then
            return false
        end
        valueRef5.value, p = true, valueRef2.value
        p = p(r)
        p = {value = p}
        if (not p.value) then
            valueRef5.value = false
            return false
        end
        k = {value = contextRef.value.lp.Character}
        if (not k.value) then
            valueRef5.value = false
            return false
        end
        _, h = callbacks.invokeCreateHumanoidModelFromUserId(contextRef, p), pcall
        h, _ = h(_)
        l = (not h)
        if h then
            l = (not _)
        end
        if l then
            valueRef5.value = false
            return false
        end
        valueRef4.value(k.value)
        l = ipairs
        f = packResults(_:GetChildren())
        l, f, s = l(unpackResults(f))
        l, f, s = normalizeIterator(l, f, s)
        n, o = l(f, s)
        s = n
        if (n ~= nil) then
            while true do
                g = o:IsA("Shirt")
                if (not g) then
                    g = o:IsA("Pants")
                end
                if (not g) then
                    g = o:IsA("ShirtGraphic")
                end
                if (not g) then
                    g = o:IsA("BodyColors")
                end
                if (not g) then
                    g = o:IsA("CharacterMesh")
                end
                if (not g) then
                    g = o:IsA("TShirt")
                end
                if g then
                    o:Clone().Parent = k.value
                end
                n, o = l(f, s)
                s = n
                if (n == nil) then
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        valueRef3.value(k.value, p.value)
        l = ipairs
        f = packResults(_:GetChildren())
        l, f, s = l(unpackResults(f))
        l, f, s = normalizeIterator(l, f, s)
        n, o = l(f, s)
        s = n
        if (n ~= nil) then
            while true do
                g = o:IsA("Accessory")
                if g then
                    g = o:Clone()
                    g = {value = g}
                    b, e = callbacks.attachClonedAccessory(g, k, valueRef), pcall
                    e(b)
                end
                n, o = l(f, s)
                s = n
                if (n == nil) then
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        _:Destroy()
        valueRef6.value = r
        valueRef5.value = false
        return true
    end
end

        b = packResults(game:HttpGet(valueRef2.value))
        e, a = a, a.JSONDecode
        a = packResults(a(e, unpackResults(b)))

    end
end

callbacks.applyLighting = function(valueRef, valueRef2, contextRef)
    return function(enabled)
        local _, b, h, k, l, m
        contextRef.value.s.lightingActive = enabled
        if enabled then
            contextRef.value.Lighting.Brightness = 2
            contextRef.value.Lighting.ColorShift_Bottom = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
            contextRef.value.Lighting.ColorShift_Top = Color3.fromRGB(255, 245, 230)
            contextRef.value.Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(128, 128, 128)
            contextRef.value.Lighting.GeographicLatitude = 45
            m, b, k = pairs(valueRef2.value)
            m, b, k = normalizeIterator(m, b, k)
            h, _ = m(b, k)
            k = h
            if (h ~= nil) then
                while true do
                    l = _
                    if _ then
                        l = _.Parent
                    end
                    if l then
                        _:Destroy()
                    end
                    h, _ = m(b, k)
                    k = h
                    if (h == nil) then
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
            b = {}
            b, valueRef2.value = Instance, b
            m = b.new("BloomEffect", contextRef.value.Lighting)
            m.Name = "PX_Bloom"
            m.Intensity = 0.5
            m.Size = 24
            m.Threshold = 0.9
            table.insert(valueRef2.value, m)
            b = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect", contextRef.value.Lighting)
            b.Name = "PX_CC"
            b.Brightness = 0.05
            b.Contrast = 0.1
            b.Saturation = 0.15
            table.insert(valueRef2.value, b)
            k = Instance.new("SunRaysEffect", contextRef.value.Lighting)
            k.Name = "PX_SunRays"
            k.Intensity = 0.1
            k.Spread = 1
            table.insert(valueRef2.value, k)
        else
            k = valueRef.value
            contextRef.value.Lighting.Brightness = valueRef.value.Brightness
            contextRef.value.Lighting.ColorShift_Bottom = k.ColorShift_Bottom
            contextRef.value.Lighting.ColorShift_Top = k.ColorShift_Top
            contextRef.value.Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = k.OutdoorAmbient
            contextRef.value.Lighting.GeographicLatitude = k.GeographicLatitude
            m, b, k = pairs(valueRef2.value)
            m, b, k = normalizeIterator(m, b, k)
            h, _ = m(b, k)
            k = h
            if (h ~= nil) then
                while true do
                    l = _
                    if _ then
                        l = _.Parent
                    end
                    if l then
                        _:Destroy()
                    end
                    h, _ = m(b, k)
                    k = h
                    if (h == nil) then
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
            b = {}
            valueRef2.value = b
        end
        return
    end
end

    return function()

            wait(3)
            a = valueRef4.value
            b = valueRef.value("players")
            a, e, b = a.SetTitle, a, (b .. (#valueRef5.value:GetPlayers()))
            a(e, b)
            a, e = pcall, callbacks.updatePingLabel(valueRef, valueRef3, valueRef2)
            a(e)
            controlRef.value:Set(math.min(100, (((#valueRef5.value:GetPlayers()) / 20) * 100)))
        end
    end
end

callbacks.stripModelScripts = function()
    return function(a)
        local _, b, e, f, g, h, k
        e = ipairs
        b = packResults(a:GetDescendants())
        e, b, k = e(unpackResults(b))
        e, b, k = normalizeIterator(e, b, k)
        h, _ = e(b, k)
        k = h
        if (h ~= nil) then
            while true do
                _ = {value = _}
                g, f = pcall, callbacks.clearAppearanceTextures(_)
                g(f)
                h, _ = e(b, k)
                k = h
                if (h == nil) then
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        return
    end
end

callbacks.setRecording = function(contextRef, valueRef, valueRef2)
    return function(r)
        local _, f, g, h, k, l, n, o, p, s
        p = pairs
        k = packResults(game.CoreGui:GetChildren())
        p, k, h = p(unpackResults(k))
        p, k, h = normalizeIterator(p, k, h)
        _, l = p(k, h)
        h = _
        if (_ ~= nil) then
            while true do
                f = l:IsA("ScreenGui")
                if f then
                    f = l.Name:find("Phantom")
                    if (not f) then
                        f = l.Name:find("Wind")
                    end
                    if (not f) then
                        f = l.Name:find("Dance")
                    end
                    if (not f) then
                        f = l.Name:find("InvisibleGhost")
                    end
                end
                if f then
                    f = ipairs
                    s = packResults(l:GetDescendants())
                    f, s, n = f(unpackResults(s))
                    f, s, n = normalizeIterator(f, s, n)
                    o, g = f(s, n)
                    n = o
                    if (o ~= nil) then
                        while true do
                            valueRef2.value(g, r)
                            o, g = f(s, n)
                            n = o
                            if (o == nil) then
                                break
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                _, l = p(k, h)
                h = _
                if (_ == nil) then
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        if (not r) then
            k = {}
            valueRef.value = k
        end
        if contextRef.value.fn.updateFloatingToggleVisibility then
            contextRef.value.fn.updateFloatingToggleVisibility()
        end
        if contextRef.value.fn.updateAssistFloatingVisual then
            contextRef.value.fn.updateAssistFloatingVisual()
        end
        return
    end
end

callbacks.animateGhostModel = function(instanceRef, valueRef, valueRef2, valueRef3)
    return function()
        local _, h, l, m, p, r
        r = tick()
        while true do
            m = (tick() - r)
            if (m < valueRef2.value) then
                m, p, h = ((1 - ((tick() - r) / valueRef2.value)) * valueRef.value), instanceRef.value, valueRef3.value
                _ = CFrame.new
                l = ((math.random() - 0.5) * m)
                _ = _(l, ((math.random() - 0.5) * m), 0)
                p.CFrame = (h * _)
                task.wait(0.02)
            else
                break
            end
        end
        instanceRef.value.CFrame = valueRef3.value
        return
    end
end

callbacks.runLightningPulse = function(valueRef, valueRef2, contextRef, valueRef3, valueRef4, valueRef5)
    return function()
        local _, a, h, k, l, m, p
        a, p = valueRef4.value.bolt, valueRef2.value
        k = Vector3.new
        h = valueRef5.value:NextNumber(-6, 6)
        l = packResults(valueRef5.value:NextNumber(-6, 6))
        k = k(h, 95, unpackResults(l))
        a((p + k), p, Color3.fromRGB(180, 120, 255), 0.45)
        valueRef3.value.Brightness = 18
        p, a = valueRef3.value, contextRef.value.TS
        k = TweenInfo.new(0.3)
        h = {}
        h.Brightness = 0
        a, m = a.Create, a
        a = a(m, p, k, h)
        m, a = a, a.Play
        a(m)
        valueRef4.value.paint(valueRef.value, Color3.fromRGB(240, 230, 255), Enum.Material.Neon, 0)
        a = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect", contextRef.value.Lighting)
        a.Brightness = 0.4
        a.Saturation = -0.4
        m = contextRef.value.TS
        h = TweenInfo.new(0.35)
        _ = {}
        _.Brightness = 0
        _.Saturation = 0
        p, m = m, m.Create
        m = m(p, a, h, _)
        m, p = m.Play, m
        m(p)
        contextRef.value.Debris:AddItem(a, 0.5)
        return
    end
end

callbacks.updateFloatingToggleVisibility = function(instanceRef, contextRef)
    return function()
        local a, e
        a = (not instanceRef.value)
        if instanceRef.value then
            a = (not instanceRef.value.Parent)
        end
        if a then
            return
        end
        a = contextRef.value.s
        if contextRef.value.s then
            a = contextRef.value.s.recActive
        end
        if a then
            a = instanceRef.value
            instanceRef.value.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            a.TextTransparency = 1
            e, a = a, a.FindFirstChildOfClass
            a = a(e, "UIStroke")
            if a then
                instanceRef.value:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke").Transparency = 1
            end
            a = instanceRef.value:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGradient")
            if a then
                instanceRef.value:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGradient").Transparency = NumberSequence.new(1)
            end
        else
            a = instanceRef.value
            instanceRef.value.BackgroundTransparency = 0
            a.TextTransparency = 0
            a, e = a.FindFirstChildOfClass, a
            a = a(e, "UIStroke")
            if a then
                instanceRef.value:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke").Transparency = 0
            end
            a = instanceRef.value:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGradient")
            if a then
                instanceRef.value:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGradient").Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0)
            end
        end
        return
    end
end

callbacks.invokeClock = function(connectionRef, valueRef, valueRef2, valueRef3)
    return function(a)
        local e
        e = ((os.clock() - valueRef2.value) / valueRef.value)
        if (e >= 1) then
            connectionRef.value:Disconnect()
            pcall(valueRef3.value, 1, a)
            return
        end
        pcall(valueRef3.value, e, a)
        return
    end
end

    return function(a, m)

        k = {value = m.Position}
        h, l = task.delay, callbacks.fadeDestroyedModel(k, a, valueRef3)
        h(1, l)
        h = 1
        f = 1
        while true do
            f = {value = f}
            i = task.delay
            n, o = (0.13 * f.value), callbacks.createHeartBillboard(f, valueRef2, valueRef, valueRef3, k)
            i(n, o)
            h = (h + 1)
            f = h
            if (h > 8) then
                break
            end
        end
        return
    end
end

            end
        end

    end

callbacks.setModelTransparency = function(contextRef, valueRef, valueRef2, valueRef3, valueRef4)
    return function()
        local _, a, h, i, k, l, m, n, p
        k, a = {}, ipairs
        k[1], k[2] = valueRef4.value, valueRef.value

        k, h = a(m, p)

        if (k ~= nil) then

                _ = contextRef.value.TS
                i = TweenInfo.new(0.7)
                n = {}
                n.Transparency = 1
                _, l = _.Create, _
                _ = _(l, h, i, n)
                l, _ = _, _.Play
                _(l)
                k, h = a(m, p)
                p = k
                if (k == nil) then
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        valueRef3.value.fade(valueRef2.value, 0.7)
        return
    end
end

callbacks.findArenaOpponents = function(valueRef, contextRef)
    return function()
        local A, B, D, _, a, f, h, k, m, p, r, s, v, x, z
        r = tick()
        if ((r - valueRef.value.time) < 0.1) then
            return valueRef.value.enemies, valueRef.value.arena, valueRef.value.mySide
        end
        p = {}
        p, m, k = nil, p, nil
        h = workspace:FindFirstChild("Arenas")
        if h then
            h = pairs
            _ = packResults(workspace.Arenas:GetChildren())
            h, _, A = h(unpackResults(_))
            h, _, A = normalizeIterator(h, _, A)
            f, s = h(_, A)
            A = f
            if (f ~= nil) then
                while true do
                    z = (f == "Arena5")
                    if (f ~= "Arena5") then
                        z = (f == "Arena5ICED")
                    end
                    if z then
                        f, s = h(_, A)
                        A = f
                        if (f == nil) then
                            break
                        end
                    else
                        z = s:FindFirstChild("Slots")
                        if z then
                            z = s.Slots:FindFirstChild("Left")
                            if z then
                                z = 1
                                while true do
                                    x = s.Slots.Left
                                    B = packResults(tostring(z))
                                    x, D = x.FindFirstChild, x
                                    x = x(D, unpackResults(B))
                                    D = x
                                    if x then
                                        D = x:FindFirstChild("Data")
                                    end
                                    if D then
                                        D = x.Data:FindFirstChild("Player")
                                    end
                                    if D then
                                        if (x.Data.Player.Value == contextRef.value.lp) then
                                            p, k = "Left", s
                                            break
                                        end
                                        z = (z + 1)
                                        if (z > 4) then
                                            break
                                        end
                                    else
                                        z = (z + 1)
                                        if (z > 4) then
                                            break
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                            z = (not k)
                            if (not k) then
                                z = s.Slots:FindFirstChild("Right")
                            end
                            if z then
                                z = 1
                                while true do
                                    x = s.Slots.Right
                                    B = packResults(tostring(z))
                                    x, D = x.FindFirstChild, x
                                    x = x(D, unpackResults(B))
                                    D = x
                                    if x then
                                        D = x:FindFirstChild("Data")
                                    end
                                    if D then
                                        D = x.Data:FindFirstChild("Player")
                                    end
                                    if D then
                                        if (x.Data.Player.Value == contextRef.value.lp) then
                                            k, p = s, "Right"
                                            break
                                        end
                                        z = (z + 1)
                                        if (z > 4) then
                                            break
                                        end
                                    else
                                        z = (z + 1)
                                        if (z > 4) then
                                            break
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                            z = p
                            if p then
                                z = k
                            end
                            if z then
                                z = (p == "Left")
                                if (p == "Left") then
                                    z = "Right"
                                end
                                if (not z) then
                                    z = "Left"
                                end
                                v = k.Slots:FindFirstChild(z)
                                if v then
                                    v = 1
                                    while true do
                                        D = k.Slots[z]
                                        a = packResults(tostring(v))
                                        B, D = D, D.FindFirstChild
                                        D = D(B, unpackResults(a))
                                        B = D
                                        if D then
                                            B = D:FindFirstChild("Data")
                                        end
                                        if B then
                                            B = D.Data:FindFirstChild("Player")
                                        end
                                        if B then
                                            a = D.Data.Player.Value
                                            if D.Data.Player.Value then
                                                a = (D.Data.Player.Value ~= contextRef.value.lp)
                                            end
                                            if a then
                                                table.insert(m, D.Data.Player.Value)
                                            end
                                        end
                                        v = (v + 1)
                                        if (v > 4) then
                                            break
                                        end
                                    end
                                    break
                                end
                                break
                            end
                            f, s = h(_, A)
                            A = f
                            if (f == nil) then
                                break
                            end
                        else
                            f, s = h(_, A)
                            A = f
                            if (f == nil) then
                                break
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        _ = valueRef.value
        valueRef.value.enemies, f, A = m, valueRef.value, valueRef.value
        _.arena = k
        A.mySide = p
        f.time = r
        return m, k, p
    end
end

callbacks.prepareBombGrabCharacter = function(valueRef, contextRef, connectionRef)
    return function(a)
        local _, b, e, h, k
        e = a:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
        e = {value = e}
        b = a:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
        b = {value = b}

        if e.value then
            k = (not b.value)
        end
        if k then
            return
        end
        if connectionRef.value then
            connectionRef.value:Disconnect()
        end
        _, k = callbacks.updateVelocity(e, b, contextRef, valueRef), contextRef.value.RunService.RenderStepped
        k, h = k.Connect, k
        k = k(h, _)
        connectionRef.value = k
        return
    end
end

callbacks.predictMovingTarget = function(contextRef)
    return function(r, m)
        local h, k, l, n, o, p, s
        p = r.Position
        h = Vector3.new(r.AssemblyLinearVelocity.X, 0, r.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Z)
        k = h
        h = h.Magnitude
        if (h > 50) then
            k = (k.Unit * 50)
            l = math.clamp((((p - m.Position)).Magnitude / 42), 0.12, 0.42)
            s = RaycastParams.new()
            o = {}
            o[2], o[1] = contextRef.value.lp.Character, r.Parent
            s.FilterDescendantsInstances = o
            s.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
            n = workspace:Raycast(p, ((p + (k * l)) - p), s)
            if n then
                return (n.Position - (k.Unit * 1.2))
            end
            return (p + (k * l))
        end
        if (h < 1.5) then
            return p
        end
        l = math.clamp((((p - m.Position)).Magnitude / 42), 0.12, 0.42)
        s = RaycastParams.new()
        o = {}
        o[2], o[1] = contextRef.value.lp.Character, r.Parent
        s.FilterDescendantsInstances = o
        s.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
        n = workspace:Raycast(p, ((p + (k * l)) - p), s)
        if n then
            return (n.Position - (k.Unit * 1.2))
        end
        return (p + (k * l))
    end
end

callbacks.createEffectRing = function(valueRef, contextRef)
    return function(r, m, p, k)
        local _, b, e, f, g, h, l, n, s
        _, h = {}, valueRef.value.spawn
        _.Shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder
        f = k
        if (not k) then
            f = 0.3
        end
        _.Size = Vector3.new(f, 2, 2)
        _.Color = m
        _.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
        f = CFrame.new
        s = Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0)
        s = (r - s)
        f = f(s)
        _.CFrame = (f * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, (math.pi / 2)))
        h = h(_)
        _ = contextRef.value.TS
        s = TweenInfo.new(0.75, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
        n = {}
        g = k
        if (not k) then
            g = 0.3
        end
        e = p
        if (not p) then
            e = 22
        end
        b = p
        if (not p) then
            b = 22
        end
        n.Size = Vector3.new(g, e, b)
        n.Transparency = 1
        l, _ = _, _.Create
        _ = _(l, h, s, n)
        l, _ = _, _.Play
        _(l)
        contextRef.value.Debris:AddItem(h, 1.1)
        return
    end
end

callbacks.restoreKorbloxAppearance = function(valueRef)
    return function(r)
        local _, f, h, k, l, m, n, o, p, s
        m, p, k = pairs(valueRef.value)
        m, p, k = normalizeIterator(m, p, k)
        h, _ = m(p, k)
        k = h
        if (h ~= nil) then
            while true do
                _ = {value = _}
                l = _.value
                if _.value then
                    l = _.value.Parent
                end
                if l then
                    l, f = pcall, callbacks.invokeDestroy4(_)
                    l(f)
                end
                h, _ = m(p, k)
                k = h
                if (h == nil) then
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        p = {}
        m, valueRef.value = r.FindFirstChild, p
        m = m(r, "RightUpperLeg")
        if m then
            m.Transparency = 0
        end
        p = r:FindFirstChild("RightLowerLeg")
        if p then
            p.Transparency = 0
        end
        k = r:FindFirstChild("RightFoot")
        if k then
            k.Transparency = 0
        end
        h = r:FindFirstChild("Right Leg")
        if h then
            _ = ipairs
            l = packResults(h:GetChildren())
            _, l, f = _(unpackResults(l))
            _, l, f = normalizeIterator(_, l, f)
            s, n = _(l, f)
            f = s
            if (s ~= nil) then
                while true do
                    o = n:IsA("SpecialMesh")
                    if o then
                        o = (n.MeshId == "rbxassetid://101851696")
                    end
                    if o then
                        n:Destroy()
                    end
                    s, n = _(l, f)
                    f = s
                    if (s == nil) then
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return
    end
end

callbacks.updateBombGrabMovement = function(valueRef, valueRef2, contextRef)
    return function()
        local _, h, k, m, o, p, r, s
        if (not contextRef.value.s.grabBombActive) then
            return
        end
        r = contextRef.value.s.assistEnabled
        if contextRef.value.s.assistEnabled then
            r = contextRef.value.s.assistHasTarget
        end
        if r then
            return
        end
        r = contextRef.value.lp.Character
        m = contextRef.value.lp.Character
        if contextRef.value.lp.Character then
            m = contextRef.value.lp.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        end
        p = r
        if r then
            p = r:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        end
        k = (not r)
        if r then
            k = (not m)
        end
        if (not k) then
            k = (not p)
        end
        if (not k) then
            k = (m.Health <= 0)
        end
        if k then
            return
        end
        k = valueRef2.value(r)
        if k then
            return
        end
        k = valueRef.value()
        h = (not k)
        if k then
            h = (not k.Character)
        end
        if h then
            return
        end
        h = k.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if (not h) then
            return
        end
        _ = k.Character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
        if (not _) then
            _ = k.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
        end
        if _ then
            s = ((_.Position + (_.CFrame.LookVector * 2.5)) + (_.CFrame.RightVector * 0.3))
            o = s
            if (((p.Position - s)).Magnitude < 1.5) then
                o = (s + (_.CFrame.LookVector * 0.5))
            end
            m:MoveTo(o)
        else
            m:MoveTo(h.Position)
        end
        return
    end
end

callbacks.updateVelocity = function(valueRef, instanceRef, contextRef, valueRef2)
    return function()
        local a, m, p
        a = (not contextRef.value.s.speedActive)
        if contextRef.value.s.speedActive then
            a = (not instanceRef.value)
        end
        if (not a) then
            a = (not instanceRef.value.Parent)
        end
        if a then
            return
        end
        a = valueRef.value.MoveDirection
        if (valueRef.value.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0.01) then
            m = valueRef2.value
            p = packResults(math.clamp(contextRef.value.s.speedLevel, 1, 10))
            m = m(unpackResults(p))
            if (contextRef.value.s.speedLevel >= 7) then
                m = (m * 1.2)
            else
                if (contextRef.value.s.speedLevel >= 5) then
                    m = (m * 1.1)
                end
            end
            p = Vector3.new(a.X, 0, a.Z)
            if (p.Magnitude > 0) then
                instanceRef.value.Velocity = Vector3.new(((p.Unit * m)).X, instanceRef.value.Velocity.Y, ((p.Unit * m)).Z)
            end
        else
            instanceRef.value.Velocity = Vector3.new((instanceRef.value.Velocity.X * 0.8), instanceRef.value.Velocity.Y, (instanceRef.value.Velocity.Z * 0.8))
        end
        return
    end
end

callbacks.applyCharacterTransparency = function(instanceRef)
    return function()
        local _, a, b, h, k, m
        a = ipairs
        m = packResults(instanceRef.value:GetDescendants())
        a, m, b = a(unpackResults(m))
        a, m, b = normalizeIterator(a, m, b)
        k, h = a(m, b)
        b = k
        if (k ~= nil) then
            while true do
                _ = h:IsA("BasePart")
                if _ then
                    h.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
                    h.Transparency = 1
                else
                    _ = h:IsA("Decal")
                    if (not _) then
                        _ = h:IsA("Texture")
                    end
                    if _ then
                        h.Transparency = 1
                    else
                        _ = h:IsA("BillboardGui")
                        if _ then
                            h.Enabled = false
                        end
                    end
                end
                k, h = a(m, b)
                b = k
                if (k == nil) then
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        return
    end
end

callbacks.prepareEffectClone = function(contextRef, valueRef, valueRef2)
    return function(a, m)
        local _, f, h, i, k, l, n, p
        a = {value = a}
        p = {value = nil}
        k, h = pcall, callbacks.cloneCharacterForEffect(a, p)
        k(h)
        if (not p.value) then
            return nil
        end
        k = ipairs
        h = packResults(p.value:GetDescendants())
        k, h, _ = k(unpackResults(h))
        k, h, _ = normalizeIterator(k, h, _)
        l, f = k(h, _)
        _ = l
        if (l ~= nil) then
            while true do
                f = {value = f}
                n, i = callbacks.stripDescendantScripts(f), pcall
                i(n)
                l, f = k(h, _)
                _ = l
                if (l == nil) then
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        k = ipairs
        h = packResults(valueRef2.value.parts(p.value))
        k, h, _ = k(unpackResults(h))
        k, h, _ = normalizeIterator(k, h, _)
        l, f = k(h, _)
        _ = l
        if (l ~= nil) then
            while true do
                f.Anchored = true
                f.CanCollide = false
                f.CanTouch = false
                f.CanQuery = false
                f.Massless = true
                l, f = k(h, _)
                _ = l
                if (l == nil) then
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        if m then
            p.value:PivotTo(m)
        end
        p.value.Parent = valueRef.value
        contextRef.value.Debris:AddItem(p.value, 12)
        return p.value
    end
end

callbacks.setSpeedLevel = function(contextRef)
    return function(a)
        local b, e, h, k
        e = contextRef.value.s
        b = math.clamp
        k = math.floor
        h = tonumber(a)
        if (not h) then
            h = 5
        end
        k = k(h)
        b = b(k, 1, 10)
        e.speedLevel = b
        if contextRef.value.s.speedActive then
            contextRef.value.fn.speedSetup()
        end
        return
    end
end

    return function()

            if workspace.CurrentCamera then
                workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame = (workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0, 0, contextRef.value.s.currentStretch, 0, 0, 0, 1))
            end
        end
        return
    end
end

callbacks.selectRange = function(contextRef)
    return function(selection)
        local _, b, f, h, k, m
        m, b, k = ipairs(contextRef.value.vals.rangeValues)
        m, b, k = normalizeIterator(m, b, k)
        h, _ = m(b, k)
        k = h
        if (h ~= nil) then
            while true do
                f = tostring(_)
                if ((f .. "m") == selection) then
                    contextRef.value.s.rangeIndex = h
                    break
                end
                h, _ = m(b, k)
                k = h
                if (h == nil) then
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        return
    end
end

callbacks.selectTheme = function(windowRef)
    return function(selection)
        local b, h
        b = {}
        b["داكن 🌙"] = "Dark"
        b["فاتح ☀️"] = "Light"
        b["وردي 🌸"] = "Rose"
        b["نباتي 🌿"] = "Plant"
        b["أحمر 🔴"] = "Red"
        b["نيلي 🔵"] = "Indigo"
        b["سماء ☁️"] = "Sky"
        b["بنفسجي 🟣"] = "Violet"
        b["زمردي 💚"] = "Emerald"
        b["منتصف الليل 🌙"] = "Midnight"
        b["قرمزي 🩸"] = "Crimson"
        h = b[selection]
        if (not b[selection]) then
            h = "Dark"
        end
        windowRef.value:SetTheme(h)
        return
    end
end

callbacks.selectCameraMultiplier = function(contextRef)
    return function(selection)
        local _, b, f, h, k, m
        m, b, k = ipairs(contextRef.value.vals.camMultValues)
        m, b, k = normalizeIterator(m, b, k)
        h, _ = m(b, k)
        k = h
        if (h ~= nil) then
            while true do
                f = tostring(_)
                if ((f .. "x") == selection) then
                    contextRef.value.s.camMultIndex = h
                    break
                end
                h, _ = m(b, k)
                k = h
                if (h == nil) then
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        contextRef.value.fn.applyCamSensitivity()
        return
    end
end

callbacks.disconnectAllConnections = function()
    return function()
        local _, a, b, e, h, k, l
        if _G._PhantomX_P then
            e = _G._PhantomX_P.conns
            if (not _G._PhantomX_P.conns) then
                k = {}
                e = k
            end
            a, e, b = ipairs(e)
            a, e, b = normalizeIterator(a, e, b)
            k, h = a(e, b)
            b = k
            if (k ~= nil) then
                while true do
                    h = {value = h}
                    l, _ = callbacks.disconnectStoredConnection(h), pcall
                    _(l)
                    k, h = a(e, b)
                    b = k
                    if (k == nil) then
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
            b = {}
            _G._PhantomX_P.conns = b
        end
        return
    end
end

callbacks.createLightningBolt = function(contextRef, valueRef, valueRef2)
    return function(r, m, p, k)
        local B, _, h, s, v, x, y, z
        _ = {}
        _[1] = r
        _, h = 1, _
        while true do
            s = table.insert
            v = r:Lerp(m, (_ / 9))
            y = Vector3.new
            x = valueRef2.value:NextNumber(-3, 3)
            B = packResults(valueRef2.value:NextNumber(-3, 3))
            y = y(x, 0, unpackResults(B))
            v = (v + y)
            s(h, v)
            _ = (_ + 1)
            if (_ > 8) then
                break
            end
        end
        table.insert(h, m)
        _ = 1
        if (not (1 > ((#h) - 1))) then
            while true do
                s = h[_]
                z = h[(_ + 1)]
                v = ((h[(_ + 1)] - h[_])).Magnitude
                y = valueRef.value.spawn
                x = {}
                x.Size = Vector3.new(k, k, ((h[(_ + 1)] - h[_])).Magnitude)
                x.Color = p
                x.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
                B = CFrame.lookAt(s, z)
                x.CFrame = (B * CFrame.new(0, 0, ((-v) / 2)))
                y = y(x)
                contextRef.value.Debris:AddItem(y, 0.6)
                _ = (_ + 1)
                if (_ > ((#h) - 1)) then
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        return
    end
end

callbacks.createEffectShards = function(contextRef, valueRef, valueRef2)
    return function(r, m, p, k, h, _)
        local A, B, a, b, d, f, i, u, v, x, y, z
        f = {}
        A = {value = f}
        f = 1
        if (not (1 > k)) then
            while true do
                v = valueRef2.value:NextNumber(0.3, 0.9)
                y, x = valueRef.value.spawn, {}
                b = Vector3.new
                a = (v * valueRef2.value:NextNumber(0.6, 1.4))
                i = valueRef2.value:NextNumber(0.6, 1.4)
                i = (v * i)
                b = b(v, a, i)
                x.Size = b
                x.Color = m
                x.Material = p
                B = CFrame.new
                a = Vector3.new
                i = valueRef2.value:NextNumber(-1.2, 1.2)
                d = valueRef2.value:NextNumber(-2, 2)
                u = packResults(valueRef2.value:NextNumber(-1.2, 1.2))
                a = a(i, d, unpackResults(u))
                a = (r + a)
                B = B(a)
                a = CFrame.Angles
                i = valueRef2.value:NextNumber(0, 6)
                d = valueRef2.value:NextNumber(0, 6)
                u = packResults(valueRef2.value:NextNumber(0, 6))
                a = a(i, d, unpackResults(u))
                x.CFrame = (B * a)
                y = y(x)
                b = Vector3.new
                B = valueRef2.value:NextNumber(-1, 1)
                a = valueRef2.value:NextNumber(0.2, 1.2)
                i = packResults(valueRef2.value:NextNumber(-1, 1))
                b = b(B, a, unpackResults(i))
                B, x = {}, b.Unit
                B.p = y
                B.v = ((x * valueRef2.value:NextNumber(0.5, 1)) * h)
                a = Vector3.new
                i = valueRef2.value:NextNumber(-6, 6)
                d = valueRef2.value:NextNumber(-6, 6)
                u = packResults(valueRef2.value:NextNumber(-6, 6))
                a = a(i, d, unpackResults(u))
                B.w = a
                A.value[f] = B
                contextRef.value.Debris:AddItem(y, (_ + 0.5))
                f = (f + 1)
                if (f > k) then
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        f, z = valueRef.value.anim, callbacks.animateFlyingShard(A)
        f(_, z)
        return
    end
end

callbacks.readNumberFromGui = function()
    return function(a)
        local _, f, h, i, k, l, m, p
        if (not a) then
            return nil
        end
        m = ipairs
        p = packResults(a:GetDescendants())
        m, p, k = m(unpackResults(p))
        m, p, k = normalizeIterator(m, p, k)
        h, _ = m(p, k)
        k = h
        if (h == nil) then
            return nil
        end
        while true do
            l = _:IsA("TextLabel")
            if (not l) then
                l = _:IsA("TextBox")
            end
            if l then
                l = tonumber
                i = _.Text
                if (not _.Text) then
                    i = ""
                end
                f = packResults(string.match(i, "%d+%.?%d*"))
                l = l(unpackResults(f))
                if l then
                    return l
                end
                h, _ = m(p, k)
                k = h
                if (h == nil) then
                    return nil
                end
            else
                h, _ = m(p, k)
                k = h
                if (h == nil) then
                    return nil
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

        local _, f, h, k, l, n, p

        p = {value = m.Position}
        k = {value = (p.value.Y - 2.6)}

        _.Shape = Enum.PartType.Ball
        _.Size = (Vector3.one * 1.5)
        _.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 190, 60)
        _.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
        l = CFrame.new
        f = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
        f = (p.value + f)
        l = l(f)
        _.CFrame = l
        h = h(_)
        h = {value = h}
        _ = Instance.new("PointLight", h.value)
        _ = {value = _}
        _.value.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 180, 60)
        _.value.Range = 20
        _.value.Brightness = 8
        l = {value = 0}

callbacks.effectGolden = function(valueRef)
    return function(r, m)
        local _, f, g, h, k, l, p, s, v, w, x
        r = {value = r}
        valueRef.value.strip(r.value)
        p = valueRef.value.parts(r.value)
        valueRef.value.paint(p, Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 50), Enum.Material.Metal, 0.5, 0.5)
        k = Instance.new("PointLight", m)
        k.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 210, 70)
        k.Range = 20
        k.Brightness = 3
        h, _, l = ipairs(p)
        h, _, l = normalizeIterator(h, _, l)
        f, s = h(_, l)
        l = f
        if (f ~= nil) then
            while true do
                w = (s.Name == "Torso")
                if (s.Name ~= "Torso") then
                    w = (s.Name == "UpperTorso")
                end
                if (not w) then
                    w = (s.Name == "Head")
                end
                if w then
                    w = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter", s)
                    v = ColorSequence.new
                    g = Color3.fromRGB(255, 230, 120)
                    x = packResults(Color3.fromRGB(255, 180, 30))
                    v = v(g, unpackResults(x))
                    w.Color = v
                    w.Size = NumberSequence.new(0.7, 0)
                    w.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(1, 1.6)
                    w.Rate = 40
                    w.Speed = NumberRange.new(4, 8)
                    w.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(180, 180)
                    w.LightEmission = 1
                    w.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0, 1)
                end
                f, s = h(_, l)
                l = f
                if (f == nil) then
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        h = r.value:GetPivot()
        h = {value = h}
        f, _ = callbacks.invokeNew2(r, h), valueRef.value.anim
        _(3, f)
        _, f = task.delay, callbacks.invokeFade2(valueRef, r)
        _(2.4, f)
        return
    end
end

        end
        return
    end

        instanceRef.value.Size = (Vector3.one * (1.5 * b))
        _ = math.sin(valueRef2.value)
        h = (_ > 0)
        if (_ > 0) then
            h = Color3.fromRGB(255, 190, 60)
        end
        if (not h) then
            h = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        end
        k.Color = h
        valueRef.value.Brightness = (8 + (a * 22))
        return
    end

callbacks.paintModel = function(contextRef)
    return function(r, m, p, k, h)
        local _, b, f, g, l, s, t, v, w
        _, l, f = ipairs(r)
        _, l, f = normalizeIterator(_, l, f)
        s, w = _(l, f)
        f = s
        if (s ~= nil) then
            while true do
                if (w.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart") then
                    w.Material = p
                    v = k
                    if (not k) then
                        v = 0
                    end
                    w.Reflectance = v
                    if h then
                        v = contextRef.value.TS
                        b = TweenInfo.new(h)
                        t = {}
                        t.Color = m
                        v, g = v.Create, v
                        v = v(g, w, b, t)
                        g, v = v, v.Play
                        v(g)
                    else
                        w.Color = m
                    end
                end
                s, w = _(l, f)
                f = s
                if (s == nil) then
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        return
    end
end

        a = a()
        if ((a - valueRef2.value) >= 1) then
            m = math.floor((valueRef3.value / (a - valueRef2.value)))
            h, valueRef2.value, valueRef3.value, b = valueRef4.value, a, 0, valueRef.value

callbacks.animateBalloon = function(valueRef, valueRef2, valueRef3, valueRef4)
    return function(r, m)
        local A, _, a, f, h, i, k, p, v, x, z
        k = {}
        p = k
        k, h, _ = pairs(valueRef3.value)
        k, h, _ = normalizeIterator(k, h, _)
        A = k(h, _)
        _ = A
        if (A ~= nil) then
            while true do
                p[A] = A.Position
                A = k(h, _)
                _ = A
                if (A == nil) then
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        k, h, _ = ipairs(valueRef2.value)
        k, h, _ = normalizeIterator(k, h, _)
        A, f = k(h, _)
        _ = A
        if (A ~= nil) then
            while true do
                z = p[valueRef.value[f]]
                v = valueRef4.value[valueRef.value[f]]
                x = CFrame.new((valueRef3.value[valueRef.value[f]] * m))
                i = CFrame.new(z)
                a = (i * CFrame.Angles((v.X * m), (v.Y * m), (v.Z * m)))
                f.CFrame = (x * ((a * CFrame.new((-z))) * f.CFrame))
                A, f = k(h, _)
                _ = A
                if (A == nil) then
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        return
    end
end

            end
            if e then
                valueRef.value()

                break
            end
        end
        return
    end

callbacks.getEligibleOpponentRoots = function(contextRef)
    return function()
        local A, _, a, b, d, f, h, i, k, m, o, p, r, s, t, u, v, x, y, z
        r = contextRef.value.lp.Character
        if contextRef.value.lp.Character then
            r = contextRef.value.lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        end
        if (not r) then
            return nil, math.huge
        end
        m = nil
        p = math.huge
        k = workspace:FindFirstChild("Arenas")
        if k then
            k = pairs
            h = packResults(workspace.Arenas:GetChildren())
            k, h, _ = k(unpackResults(h))
            k, h, _ = normalizeIterator(k, h, _)
            A, f = k(h, _)
            _ = A
            if (A ~= nil) then
                while true do
                    s = (A == "Arena5")
                    if (A ~= "Arena5") then
                        s = (A == "Arena5ICED")
                    end
                    if s then
                        A, f = k(h, _)
                        _ = A
                        if (A == nil) then
                            break
                        end
                    else
                        s = f:FindFirstChild("Slots")
                        if s then
                            z, s, x = ipairs, nil, {}
                            x[1], x[2] = "Left", "Right"
                            z, v, y = z(x)
                            z, v, y = normalizeIterator(z, v, y)
                            x, b = z(v, y)
                            y = x
                            if (x ~= nil) then
                                while true do
                                    t = f.Slots:FindFirstChild(b)
                                    if t then
                                        t = 1
                                        while true do
                                            d = f.Slots[b]
                                            o = packResults(tostring(t))
                                            u, d = d, d.FindFirstChild
                                            d = d(u, unpackResults(o))
                                            u = d
                                            if d then
                                                u = d:FindFirstChild("Data")
                                            end
                                            if u then
                                                u = d.Data:FindFirstChild("Player")
                                            end
                                            if u then
                                                if (d.Data.Player.Value == contextRef.value.lp) then
                                                    s = b
                                                    break
                                                end
                                                t = (t + 1)
                                                if (t > 4) then
                                                    break
                                                end
                                            else
                                                t = (t + 1)
                                                if (t > 4) then
                                                    break
                                                end
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                    x, b = z(v, y)
                                    y = x
                                    if (x == nil) then
                                        break
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                            if s then
                                z = (s == "Left")
                                if (s == "Left") then
                                    z = "Right"
                                end
                                if (not z) then
                                    z = "Left"
                                end
                                v = f.Slots:FindFirstChild(z)
                                if v then
                                    v = 1
                                    while true do
                                        b = f.Slots[z]
                                        a = packResults(tostring(v))
                                        b, t = b.FindFirstChild, b
                                        b = b(t, unpackResults(a))
                                        t = b
                                        if b then
                                            t = b:FindFirstChild("Data")
                                        end
                                        if t then
                                            t = b.Data:FindFirstChild("Player")
                                        end
                                        if t then
                                            t = b.Data.Player.Value
                                            a = b.Data.Player.Value
                                            if b.Data.Player.Value then
                                                a = (b.Data.Player.Value ~= contextRef.value.lp)
                                            end
                                            if a then
                                                a = b.Data.Player.Value.Character
                                            end
                                            if a then
                                                a = b.Data.Player.Value.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                                                i = t.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                                d = a
                                                if a then
                                                    d = (a.Health > 0)
                                                end
                                                if d then
                                                    d = i
                                                end
                                                if d then
                                                    if (((r.Position - i.Position)).Magnitude < p) then
                                                        m, p = t, ((r.Position - i.Position)).Magnitude
                                                    end
                                                end
                                            end
                                        end
                                        v = (v + 1)
                                        if (v > 4) then
                                            break
                                        end
                                    end
                                    break
                                end
                                break
                            end
                            A, f = k(h, _)
                            _ = A
                            if (A == nil) then
                                break
                            end
                        else
                            A, f = k(h, _)
                            _ = A
                            if (A == nil) then
                                break
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return m, p
    end
end

callbacks.updateTurnBoost = function(valueRef, valueRef2, valueRef3, valueRef4, contextRef)
    return function()
        local _, f, g, h, k, l, m, p, s, v, w
        if (not contextRef.value.s.shakeEnabled) then
            return
        end
        if (not workspace.CurrentCamera) then
            return
        end
        m, p = workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame:ToEulerAnglesYXZ()
        h = valueRef2.value()
        if (not h) then
            if contextRef.value.s.shakeActive then
                valueRef.value()
            end
            _ = {}
            contextRef.value.vals.recentChanges = _
            contextRef.value.s.lastDir = 0
            valueRef4.value = p
            return
        end
        if (valueRef4.value == nil) then
            valueRef4.value = p
            return
        end
        k = (p - valueRef4.value)
        if ((p - valueRef4.value) > math.pi) then
            k = ((p - valueRef4.value) - (2 * math.pi))
        end
        if (k < (-math.pi)) then
            k = (k + (2 * math.pi))
        end
        h = math.abs(k)
        if (h > 0.04) then
            contextRef.value.s.lastChangeTime = tick()
            h = (contextRef.value.s.lastDir ~= 0)
            if (contextRef.value.s.lastDir ~= 0) then
                h = ((k * contextRef.value.s.lastDir) < 0)
            end
            if h then
                h = table.insert
                _ = contextRef.value.vals.recentChanges
                l = packResults(tick())
                h(_, unpackResults(l))
            end
            contextRef.value.s.lastDir = k
            h = tick()
            l = {}
            _, l = l, 0
            f, s, w = ipairs(contextRef.value.vals.recentChanges)
            f, s, w = normalizeIterator(f, s, w)
            v, g = f(s, w)
            w = v
            if (v ~= nil) then
                while true do
                    if ((h - g) <= 0.4) then
                        l = (l + 1)
                        table.insert(_, g)
                    end
                    v, g = f(s, w)
                    w = v
                    if (v == nil) then
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
            contextRef.value.vals.recentChanges = _
            f = (l >= 3)
            if (l >= 3) then
                f = (not contextRef.value.s.shakeActive)
            end
            if f then
                valueRef3.value()
            end
        else
            h = contextRef.value.s.shakeActive
            if contextRef.value.s.shakeActive then
                h = ((tick() - contextRef.value.s.lastChangeTime) >= 0.5)
            end
            if h then
                valueRef.value()
                l = {}
                contextRef.value.vals.recentChanges = l
            end
        end
        valueRef4.value = p
        return
    end
end

callbacks.effectChrome = function(valueRef)
    return function(r, m)
        local _, f, h, k, l, n, o, p, s
        r = {value = r}
        valueRef.value.strip(r.value)
        p = valueRef.value.parts(r.value)
        valueRef.value.paint(p, Color3.fromRGB(210, 215, 230), Enum.Material.Metal, 0.7, 0.4)
        k, h, _ = ipairs(p)
        k, h, _ = normalizeIterator(k, h, _)
        l, f = k(h, _)
        _ = l
        if (l ~= nil) then
            while true do
                s = (f.Name == "Torso")
                if (f.Name ~= "Torso") then
                    s = (f.Name == "UpperTorso")
                end
                if (not s) then
                    s = (f.Name == "Head")
                end
                if s then
                    s = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter", f)
                    n = ColorSequence.new
                    o = packResults(Color3.fromRGB(220, 225, 245))
                    n = n(unpackResults(o))
                    s.Color = n
                    s.Size = NumberSequence.new(0.5, 0)
                    s.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.6, 1.2)
                    s.Rate = 35
                    s.Speed = NumberRange.new(4, 9)
                    s.LightEmission = 1
                    s.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(180, 180)
                    s.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0, 1)
                end
                l, f = k(h, _)
                _ = l
                if (l == nil) then
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        k = {value = m.Position}
        l, h = callbacks.finishModelEffect(r, k, valueRef), task.delay
        h(2, l)
        return
    end
end

callbacks.updateSpeed = function(valueRef, instanceRef, valueRef2, instanceRef2, contextRef, valueRef3, valueRef4, valueRef5, instanceRef3, valueRef6, valueRef7)
    return function()
        local _, f, h, k, l, m, p, r, s
        if (not contextRef.value.s.wallSlideEnabled) then
            return
        end
        r = (not instanceRef3.value)
        if instanceRef3.value then
            r = (not instanceRef3.value.Parent)
        end
        if (not r) then
            r = (not valueRef6.value)
        end
        if (not r) then
            r = (valueRef6.value.Health <= 0)
        end
        if (not r) then
            r = (not instanceRef2.value)
        end
        if (not r) then
            r = (not instanceRef2.value.Parent)
        end
        if r then
            valueRef5.value()
            return
        end
        m = {}
        r, m = m, ipairs
        p = packResults(contextRef.value.Players:GetPlayers())
        m, p, k = m(unpackResults(p))
        m, p, k = normalizeIterator(m, p, k)
        h, _ = m(p, k)
        k = h
        if (h ~= nil) then
            while true do
                if _.Character then
                    table.insert(r, _.Character)
                end
                h, _ = m(p, k)
                k = h
                if (h == nil) then
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        valueRef7.value.FilterDescendantsInstances = r
        m = valueRef4.value(valueRef6.value.MoveDirection)
        if (m.Magnitude < 0.08) then
            valueRef5.value()
            return
        end
        p = valueRef2.value(m)
        if (not p) then
            valueRef5.value()
            return
        end
        k = instanceRef2.value.Velocity
        h = (valueRef6.value.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air)
        if (valueRef6.value.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air) then
            h = 0
        end
        if (not h) then
            h = math.min(instanceRef2.value.Velocity.Y, 1.5)
        end
        _ = math.abs((h - k.Y))
        if (_ > 0.01) then
            instanceRef2.value.Velocity = Vector3.new(k.X, h, k.Z)
        end
        _ = (not instanceRef.value)
        if instanceRef.value then
            _ = (not instanceRef.value.Parent)
        end
        if _ then
            instanceRef.value = valueRef3.value()
        end
        if (not instanceRef.value) then
            return
        end
        _ = valueRef4.value((m - (p * m:Dot(p))))
        l = (_.Magnitude > 0.05)
        if (_.Magnitude > 0.05) then
            l = _
        end
        if (not l) then
            l = valueRef.value
            f = Vector3.new((-p.Z), 0, p.X)
            s = packResults(Vector3.new(1, 0, 0))
            l = l(f, unpackResults(s))
        end
        f = ((l * valueRef6.value.WalkSpeed) + (p * 0.01))
        s = instanceRef.value:IsA("LinearVelocity")
        if s then
            instanceRef.value.VectorVelocity = Vector3.new(f.X, 0, f.Z)
        else
            s = instanceRef.value:IsA("BodyVelocity")
            if s then
                instanceRef.value.Velocity = Vector3.new(f.X, 0, f.Z)
            end
        end
        valueRef6.value.AutoRotate = true
        return
    end
end

callbacks.updateTurnDirection = function(contextRef)
    return function(a)
        local h, k, m, p
        if (not contextRef.value.s.turnBoost) then
            return
        end
        m = contextRef.value.s.assistEnabled
        if contextRef.value.s.assistEnabled then
            m = contextRef.value.s.assistHasTarget
        end
        if m then
            return
        end
        m = contextRef.value.lp.Character
        p = contextRef.value.lp.Character
        if contextRef.value.lp.Character then
            p = contextRef.value.lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        end
        k = m
        if m then
            k = m:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
        end
        h = p
        if p then
            h = k
        end
        if h then
            h = (k.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0.1)
        end
        if h then
            p.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(p.Position, (p.Position + p.CFrame.LookVector:Lerp(k.MoveDirection, (contextRef.value.vals.turnBoostSpeeds[contextRef.value.s.turnBoostSpeedIndex] * a))))
        end
        return
    end
end

callbacks.restoreSavedAppearance = function(valueRef, valueRef2, contextRef)
    return function()
        local _, f, h, k, l, m, p, r, s
        r = nil
        m = math.huge
        p = contextRef.value.lp.Character
        if contextRef.value.lp.Character then
            p = contextRef.value.lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        end
        if (not p) then
            return nil
        end
        k, h, _ = ipairs(valueRef2.value())
        k, h, _ = normalizeIterator(k, h, _)
        l, f = k(h, _)
        _ = l
        if (l ~= nil) then
            while true do
                s = f.Character
                if f.Character then
                    s = f.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                end
                if s then
                    s = (f.Character.Humanoid.Health > 0)
                end
                if s then
                    s = valueRef.value(f.Character)
                end
                if s then
                    s = f.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if s then
                        if (((p.Position - s.Position)).Magnitude < m) then
                            r, m = f, ((p.Position - s.Position)).Magnitude
                        end
                    end
                end
                l, f = k(h, _)
                _ = l
                if (l == nil) then
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        return r
    end
end

callbacks.findBombTool = function(valueRef, contextRef)
    return function(a, m)
        local _, b, h, k, l
        b = valueRef.value
        if (not valueRef.value) then
            b = (not a)
        end
        if (not b) then
            b = (not m)
        end
        if b then
            return
        end
        b = m:FindFirstChild("Handle")
        if (not b) then
            b = m:FindFirstChildOfClass("Part")
        end
        b = {value = b}
        if (not b.value) then
            return
        end
        k = a:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        k = {value = k}
        if (not k.value) then
            return
        end
        h = contextRef.value.lp.Character
        if contextRef.value.lp.Character then
            h = contextRef.value.lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        end
        if (not h) then
            return
        end
        if (((h.Position - k.value.Position)).Magnitude < 4.5) then
            l, valueRef.value = task, true
            _, l = l.spawn, callbacks.resetShakeState(valueRef, b, k)
            _(l)
        end
        return
    end
end

    end

        a, e = a.Connect, a

        b, a = callbacks.invokePcall(valueRef2, contextRef), b.RunService.Heartbeat
        a, e = a.Connect, a

callbacks.findWallSlideDirection = function(valueRef, valueRef2, instanceRef, valueRef3)
    return function(r)
        local _, f, g, h, l, m, p, s, v, w
        if (not instanceRef.value) then
            return nil
        end
        p = {}
        m = p
        p = Vector3.new((-r.Z), 0, r.X)
        _ = {}
        _[2], _[3], _[1] = (instanceRef.value.Position + (p * 0.25)), (instanceRef.value.Position - (p * 0.25)), instanceRef.value.Position
        h, l = _, {}
        s = valueRef2.value((r + (p * 0.25)), r)
        l[1], l[3], l[2] = r, valueRef2.value((r - (p * 0.25)), r), s
        l, _ = 1, l
        while true do
            w = workspace:Raycast(h[l], (_[l] * 1.35), valueRef.value)
            v = w
            if w then
                v = w.Instance
            end
            if v then
                v = w.Instance:IsA("BasePart")
            end
            if v then
                v = w.Instance.CanCollide
            end
            if v then
                v = (math.abs(w.Normal.Y) <= 0.3)
            end
            if v then
                v = valueRef3.value(w.Normal)
                g = (v.Magnitude > 0.001)
                if (v.Magnitude > 0.001) then
                    g = (r:Dot(v) < -0.01)
                end
                if g then
                    table.insert(m, v)
                end
            end
            l = (l + 1)
            if (l > 3) then
                break
            end
        end
        if ((#m) == 0) then
            return nil
        end
        l = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        f, s, w = ipairs(m)
        f, s, w = normalizeIterator(f, s, w)
        v, g = f(s, w)
        w = v
        if (v ~= nil) then
            while true do
                l = (l + g)
                v, g = f(s, w)
                w = v
                if (v == nil) then
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        return valueRef2.value(l, m[1])
    end
end

        end

    end

callbacks.effectFire = function(valueRef)
    return function(r, m)
        local _, f, h, k, l, p, s

        valueRef.value.strip(r.value)

        k, h, _ = ipairs(p)
        k, h, _ = normalizeIterator(k, h, _)
        l, f = k(h, _)
        _ = l
        if (l ~= nil) then
            while true do
                s = (f.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart")
                if (f.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart") then
                    s = (f.Size.Magnitude > 1)
                end
                if s then
                    s = Instance.new("Fire", f)
                    s.Size = 8
                    s.Heat = 12
                    s.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 90, 15)
                    s.SecondaryColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 20, 60)
                end
                l, f = k(h, _)
                _ = l
                if (l == nil) then
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        k = Instance.new("Smoke", m.value)
        k.Size = 8
        k.Opacity = 0.4
        k.RiseVelocity = 6
        k.Color = Color3.fromRGB(35, 32, 30)
        h = Instance.new("PointLight", m.value)
        h.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 100, 30)
        h.Range = 20
        h.Brightness = 3
        f, _ = callbacks.invokeRing(valueRef, m), task.delay
        _(1.4, f)
        f, _ = callbacks.invokeFade4(valueRef, r), task.delay
        _(2.5, f)
        return
    end
end

            end

            end
            instanceRef.value.Text = h
            instanceRef.value.TextColor3 = p
        end
        k, _ = valueRef.value, instanceRef2.value
        l = TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
        s, f = r, {}
        if r then
            s = Color3.fromRGB(20, 35, 29)
        end
        if (not s) then

        end
        f.BackgroundColor3 = s

            h, k = k, k.Play
            k(h)
        end
        if instanceRef3.value then
            k, _ = valueRef.value, instanceRef3.value
            l = TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
            f = {}
            f.BackgroundColor3 = m

            end

callbacks.clearAppearanceAccessories = function()
    return function(a, m)
        local _, f, h, i, k, l, p
        p = ipairs
        k = packResults(a:GetChildren())
        p, k, h = p(unpackResults(k))
        p, k, h = normalizeIterator(p, k, h)
        _, l = p(k, h)
        h = _
        if (_ == nil) then
            return
        end
        while true do
            f = l:IsA("BasePart")
            if f then
                f = l:FindFirstChild(m)
                i = f
                if f then
                    i = f:IsA("Attachment")
                end
                if i then
                    return l, f
                end
                _, l = p(k, h)
                h = _
                if (_ == nil) then
                    return
                end
            else
                _, l = p(k, h)
                h = _
                if (_ == nil) then
                    return
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

callbacks.spawnBlastWave = function(contextRef, valueRef, valueRef2)
    return function(r, m, p, k, h)
        local _, f, l, n, o
        _, l = valueRef2.value.spawn, {}
        l.Shape = Enum.PartType.Ball
        l.Size = (Vector3.one * m)
        l.Color = r
        l.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
        l.Transparency = h
        l.CFrame = CFrame.new((valueRef.value + Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)))
        _ = _(l)
        l = contextRef.value.TS
        n = TweenInfo.new(k, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
        o = {}
        o.Size = (Vector3.one * p)
        o.Transparency = 1
        l, f = l.Create, l
        l = l(f, _, n, o)
        l, f = l.Play, l
        l(f)
        contextRef.value.Debris:AddItem(_, (k + 0.2))
        return
    end
end

callbacks.applyAntiLag = function(valueRef, valueRef2, valueRef3, valueRef4, contextRef, valueRef5, valueRef6)
    return function(enabled)
        local _, e, f, g, h, k, l, m, n, p, s, v
        contextRef.value.s.antiLagActive = enabled
        if enabled then
            p, m = callbacks.invokeSetfpscap2(), pcall
            m(p)
            m = next
            p, k = workspace:GetDescendants()
            m, p, k = normalizeIterator(m, p, k)
            h, _ = m(p, k)
            k = h
            if (h ~= nil) then
                while true do
                    valueRef2.value(_)
                    h, _ = m(p, k)
                    k = h
                    if (h == nil) then
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
            m = next
            p, k = contextRef.value.Lighting:GetDescendants()
            m, p, k = normalizeIterator(m, p, k)
            h, _ = m(p, k)
            k = h
            if (h ~= nil) then
                while true do
                    valueRef2.value(_)
                    h, _ = m(p, k)
                    k = h
                    if (h == nil) then
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
            p, _ = {}, contextRef.value
            p.GlobalShadows = contextRef.value.Lighting.GlobalShadows
            p.FogEnd = _.Lighting.FogEnd
            p.Brightness = _.Lighting.Brightness
            valueRef5.value = p
            _.Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
            _.Lighting.FogEnd = 9000000000
            m, k = workspace.DescendantAdded, callbacks.applyAntiLagToNewObject(contextRef, valueRef2)
            p, m = m, m.Connect
            m = m(p, k)
            table.insert(valueRef4.value, m)
        else
            m, p, k = pairs(valueRef4.value)
            m, p, k = normalizeIterator(m, p, k)
            h, _ = m(p, k)
            k = h
            if (h ~= nil) then
                while true do
                    if _ then
                        _:Disconnect()
                    end
                    h, _ = m(p, k)
                    k = h
                    if (h == nil) then
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
            p = {}
            valueRef4.value, p, m = p, valueRef3.value, pairs
            m, p, k = m(p)
            m, p, k = normalizeIterator(m, p, k)
            h, _ = m(p, k)
            k = h
            if (h ~= nil) then
                while true do
                    h = {value = h}
                    f = typeof(h.value)
                    l = (f == "Instance")
                    if (f == "Instance") then
                        l = h.value.Parent
                    end
                    if l then
                        l, f, s = pairs(_)
                        l, f, s = normalizeIterator(l, f, s)
                        n, v = l(f, s)
                        s = n
                        if (n ~= nil) then
                            while true do
                                n = {value = n}
                                v = {value = v}
                                e, g = callbacks.assignStoredEntrySafely(v, n, h), pcall
                                g(e)
                                n, v = l(f, s)
                                s = n
                                if (n == nil) then
                                    break
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    h, _ = m(p, k)
                    k = h
                    if (h == nil) then
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
            p = {}
            valueRef3.value = p
            p, m, valueRef.value = valueRef6.value, pairs, 0
            m, p, k = m(p)
            m, p, k = normalizeIterator(m, p, k)
            h, _ = m(p, k)
            k = h
            if (h ~= nil) then
                while true do
                    h = {value = h}
                    _ = {value = _}
                    f = typeof(h.value)
                    l = (f == "Instance")
                    if (f == "Instance") then
                        l = _.value
                    end
                    if l then
                        f, l = callbacks.setStoredParent(h, _), pcall
                        l(f)
                    end
                    h, _ = m(p, k)
                    k = h
                    if (h == nil) then
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
            p = {}
            m, valueRef6.value = valueRef5.value, p
            if m then
                k = valueRef5.value
                contextRef.value.Lighting.GlobalShadows = valueRef5.value.GlobalShadows
                contextRef.value.Lighting.FogEnd = k.FogEnd
                contextRef.value.Lighting.Brightness = k.Brightness
                valueRef5.value = nil
            end
            p, m = callbacks.invokeSetfpscap(), pcall
            m(p)
        end
        return
    end
end
