callbacks.selectReactionTime = function(contextRef)
    return function(selection)
        local _, b, e, f, h, k, l
        e, b, k = ipairs(contextRef.value.vals.reactionTimeValues)
        e, b, k = normalizeIterator(e, b, k)
        h, _ = e(b, k)
        k = h
        if (h ~= nil) then
            while true do
                l = string.format("%.1fs", _)
                if (l == selection) then
                    f = contextRef.value
                    contextRef.value.s.reactionTimeIndex = h
                    f.s.reactionTime = _
                    f.s.reactionTargetId = nil
                    contextRef.value.s.reactionStartTime = 0
                    break
                end
                h, _ = e(b, k)
                k = h
                if (h == nil) then
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        return
    end
end

callbacks.fadeEffect = function(contextRef)
    return function(r, m)
        local _, f, g, h, k, l, p, s, v
        p = ipairs
        k = packResults(r:GetDescendants())
        p, k, h = p(unpackResults(k))
        p, k, h = normalizeIterator(p, k, h)
        _, l = p(k, h)
        h = _
        if (_ ~= nil) then
            while true do
                f = l:IsA("BasePart")
                if f then
                    f = contextRef.value.TS
                    v = TweenInfo.new(m)
                    g = {}
                    g.Transparency = 1
                    s, f = f, f.Create
                    f = f(s, l, v, g)
                    f, s = f.Play, f
                    f(s)
                else
                    f = l:IsA("Decal")
                    if (not f) then
                        f = l:IsA("Texture")
                    end
                    if f then
                        f = contextRef.value.TS
                        v = TweenInfo.new(m)
                        g = {}
                        g.Transparency = 1
                        f, s = f.Create, f
                        f = f(s, l, v, g)
                        s, f = f, f.Play
                        f(s)
                    else
                        f = l:IsA("Highlight")
                        if f then
                            f = contextRef.value.TS
                            v = TweenInfo.new(m)
                            g = {}
                            g.FillTransparency = 1
                            g.OutlineTransparency = 1
                            f, s = f.Create, f
                            f = f(s, l, v, g)
                            f, s = f.Play, f
                            f(s)
                        else
                            f = l:IsA("PointLight")
                            if f then
                                f = contextRef.value.TS
                                v = TweenInfo.new(m)
                                g = {}
                                g.Brightness = 0
                                s, f = f, f.Create
                                f = f(s, l, v, g)
                                f, s = f.Play, f
                                f(s)
                            else
                                f = l:IsA("ParticleEmitter")
                                if (not f) then
                                    f = l:IsA("Fire")
                                end
                                if (not f) then
                                    f = l:IsA("Smoke")
                                end
                                if f then
                                    l.Enabled = false
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                _, l = p(k, h)
                h = _
                if (_ == nil) then
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        return
    end
end

callbacks.stripDescendantScripts = function(windowRef)
    return function()
        local a
        a = windowRef.value:IsA("Script")
        if (not a) then
            a = windowRef.value:IsA("LocalScript")
        end
        if (not a) then
            a = windowRef.value:IsA("Humanoid")
        end
        if (not a) then
            a = windowRef.value:IsA("Sound")
        end
        if (not a) then
            a = windowRef.value:IsA("BillboardGui")
        end
        if (not a) then
            a = windowRef.value:IsA("ForceField")
        end
        if (not a) then
            a = windowRef.value:IsA("Tool")
        end
        if (not a) then
            a = windowRef.value:IsA("Highlight")
        end
        if (not a) then
            a = windowRef.value:IsA("ParticleEmitter")
        end
        if (not a) then
            a = windowRef.value:IsA("Fire")
        end
        if (not a) then
            a = windowRef.value:IsA("Smoke")
        end
        if (not a) then
            a = windowRef.value:IsA("Sparkles")
        end
        if a then
            windowRef.value:Destroy()
        end
        return
    end
end

callbacks.invokeNew = function(valueRef, instanceRef, valueRef2, valueRef3, valueRef4)
    return function(r, m)
        local h, k, p
        k = valueRef4.value
        h = Vector3.new(0, (-80 * m), 0)
        h, valueRef4.value = instanceRef.value, (k + h)
        p = (h.Position + (valueRef4.value * m))
        if (((h.Position + (valueRef4.value * m))).Y < valueRef.value) then
            p = Vector3.new(((h.Position + (valueRef4.value * m))).X, valueRef.value, ((h.Position + (valueRef4.value * m))).Z)
            h, valueRef4.value = valueRef3.value, Vector3.new((valueRef4.value.X * 0.55), (math.abs(valueRef4.value.Y) * 0.3), (valueRef4.value.Z * 0.55))
            valueRef3.value = (h * 0.6)
        end
        h = (CFrame.new(p) * (instanceRef.value - instanceRef.value.Position))
        k, instanceRef.value = valueRef2.value, (h * CFrame.Angles((valueRef3.value.X * m), (valueRef3.value.Y * m), (valueRef3.value.Z * m)))
        k, h = k.PivotTo, k
        k(h, instanceRef.value)
        return
    end
end

callbacks.endAssistDrag = function(valueRef, valueRef2)

            e = (a == valueRef.value)
        end
        if e then
            valueRef2.value = false
            valueRef.value = nil
        end
        return
    end
end

        return
    end
end

callbacks.selectPower = function(contextRef)
    return function(selection)
        local _, f, h, i, k, l, m, n, p
        p = math.clamp
        k = tonumber(selection)
        if (not k) then
            k = 45
        end
        p = p(k, 35, 55)
        m, p = (p / 100), 1
        k = math.huge
        h, _, l = ipairs(contextRef.value.vals.powerValues)
        h, _, l = normalizeIterator(h, _, l)
        f, i = h(_, l)
        l = f
        if (f ~= nil) then
            while true do
                n = math.abs((i - m))
                if (n < k) then
                    p, k = f, n
                end
                f, i = h(_, l)
                l = f
                if (f == nil) then
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        contextRef.value.s.powerIndex = p
        return
    end
end

        end
        if e then
            e, valueRef2.value = a.Position, true
            b, valueRef6.value = valueRef3.value, e
            valueRef.value, valueRef4.value = a, b.Position
        end

    end

callbacks.clearCharacterAppearance = function()
    return function(r)
        local _, f, h, k, l, m, p
        m = ipairs
        p = packResults(r:GetChildren())
        m, p, k = m(unpackResults(p))
        m, p, k = normalizeIterator(m, p, k)
        h, _ = m(p, k)
        k = h
        if (h ~= nil) then
            while true do
                l = _:IsA("Accessory")
                if (not l) then
                    l = _:IsA("Shirt")
                end
                if (not l) then
                    l = _:IsA("Pants")
                end
                if (not l) then
                    l = _:IsA("ShirtGraphic")
                end
                if (not l) then
                    l = _:IsA("BodyColors")
                end
                if (not l) then
                    l = _:IsA("CharacterMesh")
                end
                if (not l) then
                    l = _:IsA("TShirt")
                end
                if l then
                    _:Destroy()
                end
                h, _ = m(p, k)
                k = h
                if (h == nil) then
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        m = r:FindFirstChild("Head")
        if m then
            p = ipairs
            k = packResults(m:GetChildren())
            p, k, h = p(unpackResults(k))
            p, k, h = normalizeIterator(p, k, h)
            _, l = p(k, h)
            h = _
            if (_ ~= nil) then
                while true do
                    f = l:IsA("Decal")
                    if (not f) then
                        f = l:IsA("SurfaceAppearance")
                    end
                    if f then
                        l:Destroy()
                    end
                    _, l = p(k, h)
                    h = _
                    if (_ == nil) then
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return
    end
end

callbacks.updateShake = function(contextRef, valueRef, valueRef2, valueRef3)
    return function(r)
        local _, h, k, l, p, s, w
        p = valueRef2.value()
        if (not p) then
            valueRef.value()
            return
        end
        p = contextRef.value.lp.Character
        if contextRef.value.lp.Character then
            p = contextRef.value.lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        end
        k = (not p)
        if p then
            k = (contextRef.value.UIS.MouseBehavior ~= Enum.MouseBehavior.LockCenter)
        end
        if k then
            return
        end
        contextRef.value.s.shakeTime = (contextRef.value.s.shakeTime + ((r * 2) * contextRef.value.vals.shakeSpeedValues[contextRef.value.s.shakeSpeedIndex]))
        w = contextRef.value
        contextRef.value.s.shakeIndex = (math.floor((contextRef.value.s.shakeTime * 60)) + 1)
        if (w.s.shakeIndex > (#valueRef3.value)) then
            contextRef.value.s.shakeIndex = 1
            contextRef.value.s.shakeTime = 0
        end
        h, l = p.CFrame.Position, workspace
        _ = workspace.CurrentCamera
        w = CFrame.new(p.CFrame.Position)
        s = (w * CFrame.Angles(0, (valueRef3.value[contextRef.value.s.shakeIndex] * 3), 0))
        l = ((s * CFrame.new((-h))) * l.CurrentCamera.CFrame)
        _.CFrame = l
        return
    end
end

callbacks.isSlotActiveForPlayer = function()
    return function(r, m)
        local _, b, f, g, h, k, l, p, v, x
        p = (not r)
        if r then
            p = (not m)
        end
        if p then
            return false
        end
        p = m:FindFirstChild("Slots")
        if p then
            _, p = {}, pairs
            _[1], _[2] = "Left", "Right"
            p, k, h = p(_)
            p, k, h = normalizeIterator(p, k, h)
            _, l = p(k, h)
            h = _
            if (_ == nil) then
                return false
            end
            while true do
                f = m.Slots:FindFirstChild(l)
                if f then
                    f = 1
                    while true do
                        v = m.Slots[l]
                        x = packResults(tostring(f))
                        g, v = v, v.FindFirstChild
                        v = v(g, unpackResults(x))
                        g = v
                        if v then
                            g = v:FindFirstChild("Data")
                        end
                        if g then
                            g = v.Data:FindFirstChild("Player")
                        end
                        if g then
                            if (v.Data.Player.Value == r) then
                                g = v:FindFirstChild("Pad")
                                if g then
                                    g = v.Pad.Color
                                    b = math.abs((v.Pad.Color.R - 1))
                                    if (b < 0.01) then
                                        b = math.abs((g.G - 0.34901960784313724))
                                    end
                                    if (b < 0.01) then
                                        b = math.abs((g.B - 0.34901960784313724))
                                    end
                                    if (b < 0.01) then
                                        return false
                                    end
                                    return true
                                end
                                return true
                            end
                            f = (f + 1)
                            if (f > 4) then
                                break
                            end
                        else
                            f = (f + 1)
                            if (f > 4) then
                                break
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    _, l = p(k, h)
                    h = _
                    if (_ == nil) then
                        return false
                    end
                else
                    _, l = p(k, h)
                    h = _
                    if (_ == nil) then
                        return false
                    end
                end
            end
        else
            return false
        end
    end
end

    return function()
        contextRef.value.fn.destroyAssistFloating()
        return

callbacks.dispatchKillEffect = function(valueRef, valueRef2, valueRef3)
    return function(a, m, b)
        local _, h, k, l
        k = valueRef3.value[a]
        h = (not valueRef3.value[a])
        if valueRef3.value[a] then
            h = (not m)
        end
        if h then
            return
        end
        h = valueRef2.value(m, b)
        if (not h) then
            return
        end
        if (not b) then
            valueRef.value(m)
        end
        _ = h:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if (not _) then
            _ = h.PrimaryPart
        end
        if (not _) then
            _ = h:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
        end
        if (not _) then
            h:Destroy()
            return
        end
        l = pcall(k, h, _)
        if (not l) then
            h:Destroy()
        end
        return
    end
end

    return function(a)

            return
        end
        m = (a.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement)
        if (a.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement) then
            m = (a == valueRef2.value)
        end
        if m then
            p = instanceRef.value
            instanceRef.value.Position = UDim2.new(valueRef3.value.X.Scale, (valueRef3.value.X.Offset + ((a.Position - valueRef5.value)).X), valueRef3.value.Y.Scale, (valueRef3.value.Y.Offset + ((a.Position - valueRef5.value)).Y))
            p = p.Position
            valueRef.value = p
        end
        return
    end
end

callbacks.effectFloat = function(valueRef, valueRef2)
    return function(r, m)
        local _, a, b, f, h, i, k, l, p, s, t, v, x, y, z
        r = {value = r}
        p = valueRef.value.parts(r.value)
        p = {value = p}
        k = r.value:FindFirstChild("Head")
        f = {}
        h, f = f, {}
        f, _ = {}, f
        h = {value = h}
        _ = {value = _}
        l = {value = f}
        f, s, z = ipairs(p.value)
        f, s, z = normalizeIterator(f, s, z)
        v, y = f(s, z)
        z = v
        if (v ~= nil) then
            while true do
                b, x = k, y
                if k then
                    b = y:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Accessory")
                end
                if b then
                    x = k
                end
                h.value[y] = x
                if (not _.value[x]) then
                    b = Vector3.new
                    t = valueRef2.value:NextNumber(-4, 4)
                    a = valueRef2.value:NextNumber(5, 9)
                    i = packResults(valueRef2.value:NextNumber(-4, 4))
                    b = b(t, a, unpackResults(i))
                    _.value[x] = b
                    b = Vector3.new
                    t = valueRef2.value:NextNumber(-4, 4)
                    a = valueRef2.value:NextNumber(-4, 4)
                    i = packResults(valueRef2.value:NextNumber(-4, 4))
                    b = b(t, a, unpackResults(i))
                    l.value[x] = b
                end
                v, y = f(s, z)
                z = v
                if (v == nil) then
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        z, f = callbacks.animateBalloon(h, p, _, l), valueRef.value.anim
        f(3.4, z)
        task.delay(2.4, callbacks.invokeFade5(valueRef, r))
        return
    end
end

callbacks.showPeriodicReminder = function(contextRef)
    return function()
        local _, h, k, l, m, p, r
        m = {}
        r, m = m, 1
        p = (#contextRef.value.fn.RANDOM_SKIN_POOL)
        if (not (1 > (#contextRef.value.fn.RANDOM_SKIN_POOL))) then
            while true do
                r[m] = m
                m = (m + 1)
                if (m > p) then
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        m = (#r)
        if (not ((#r) < 2)) then
            while true do
                h = math.random(1, m)
                l = r[m]
                r[m] = r[h]
                r[h] = l
                m = (m + -1)
                if (m < 2) then
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        m, p, k = ipairs(r)
        m, p, k = normalizeIterator(m, p, k)
        h, _ = m(p, k)
        k = h
        if (h ~= nil) then
            while true do
                l = contextRef.value.fn.skinApply(contextRef.value.fn.RANDOM_SKIN_POOL[_], true)
                if l then
                    break
                end
                task.wait(0.15)
                h, _ = m(p, k)
                k = h
                if (h == nil) then
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        return
    end
end

callbacks.updateAssistTarget = function(valueRef, valueRef2, valueRef3, valueRef4, contextRef)
    return function(r)
        local _, b, f, h, k, l, m, p, t, v, y, z
        if (not contextRef.value.s.assistEnabled) then
            contextRef.value.s.assistHasTarget = false
            contextRef.value.s.reactionTargetId = nil
            contextRef.value.s.reactionStartTime = 0
            return
        end
        m = contextRef.value.s.grabBombActive
        if (not contextRef.value.s.grabBombActive) then
            m = contextRef.value.s.autoHoldActive
        end
        if m then
            return
        end
        m = contextRef.value.lp.Character
        if (not contextRef.value.lp.Character) then
            contextRef.value.s.assistHasTarget = false
            return
        end
        p = contextRef.value.lp.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
        if (not p) then
            contextRef.value.s.assistHasTarget = false
            contextRef.value.s.reactionTargetId = nil
            return
        end
        k = m:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        h = m:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        _ = (not k)
        if k then
            _ = (not h)
        end
        if (not _) then
            _ = (k.Health <= 0)
        end
        if _ then
            contextRef.value.s.assistHasTarget = false
            contextRef.value.s.reactionTargetId = nil
            return
        end
        _, l = valueRef2.value(h, contextRef.value.vals.rangeValues[contextRef.value.s.rangeIndex])
        f = (not _)
        if _ then
            f = (not _.Character)
        end
        if f then
            contextRef.value.s.assistHasTarget = false
            contextRef.value.s.reactionTargetId = nil
            contextRef.value.s.reactionStartTime = 0
            return
        end
        f = _.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if (not f) then
            contextRef.value.s.assistHasTarget = false
            contextRef.value.s.reactionTargetId = nil
            return
        end
        if (contextRef.value.s.reactionTargetId ~= _.UserId) then
            v = contextRef.value
            contextRef.value.s.reactionTargetId = _.UserId
            v.s.reactionStartTime = tick()
        end
        contextRef.value.s.assistHasTarget = true
        if (contextRef.value.s.reactionTime > 0) then
            z = (tick() - contextRef.value.s.reactionStartTime)
            if (z < contextRef.value.s.reactionTime) then
                return
            end
            z = valueRef.value(f, h)
            if (k.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0) then
                y = Vector3.new(((z - h.Position)).X, 0, ((z - h.Position)).Z)
                if (y.Magnitude > 0.1) then
                    t = (k.MoveDirection.Unit * (1 - contextRef.value.vals.powerValues[contextRef.value.s.powerIndex]))
                    b = (t + (y.Unit * contextRef.value.vals.powerValues[contextRef.value.s.powerIndex]))
                    t = ((t + (y.Unit * contextRef.value.vals.powerValues[contextRef.value.s.powerIndex]))).Magnitude
                    if (t > 0) then
                        k:Move(b.Unit, false)
                    end
                end
            end
            valueRef4.value(h, z, r)
            if (l < 4.5) then
                valueRef3.value(_.Character, p)
            end
            return
        end
        z = valueRef.value(f, h)
        if (k.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0) then
            y = Vector3.new(((z - h.Position)).X, 0, ((z - h.Position)).Z)
            if (y.Magnitude > 0.1) then
                t = (k.MoveDirection.Unit * (1 - contextRef.value.vals.powerValues[contextRef.value.s.powerIndex]))
                b = (t + (y.Unit * contextRef.value.vals.powerValues[contextRef.value.s.powerIndex]))
                t = ((t + (y.Unit * contextRef.value.vals.powerValues[contextRef.value.s.powerIndex]))).Magnitude
                if (t > 0) then
                    k:Move(b.Unit, false)
                end
            end
        end
        valueRef4.value(h, z, r)
        if (l < 4.5) then
            valueRef3.value(_.Character, p)
        end
        return
    end
end

callbacks.getOpposingArenaPlayers = function(contextRef)
    return function()
        local A, _, a, b, d, f, h, i, k, m, p, r, s, v, x, y, z
        m = {}
        r = m
        m = workspace:FindFirstChild("Arenas")
        if m then
            m = pairs
            p = packResults(workspace.Arenas:GetChildren())
            m, p, k = m(unpackResults(p))
            m, p, k = normalizeIterator(m, p, k)
            h, _ = m(p, k)
            k = h
            if (h ~= nil) then
                while true do
                    A = (h == "Arena5")
                    if (h ~= "Arena5") then
                        A = (h == "Arena5ICED")
                    end
                    if A then
                        h, _ = m(p, k)
                        k = h
                        if (h == nil) then
                            break
                        end
                    else
                        A = _:FindFirstChild("Slots")
                        if A then
                            v, A, f = {}, nil, ipairs
                            v[1], v[2] = "Left", "Right"
                            f, s, z = f(v)
                            f, s, z = normalizeIterator(f, s, z)
                            v, y = f(s, z)
                            z = v
                            if (v ~= nil) then
                                while true do
                                    x = _.Slots:FindFirstChild(y)
                                    if x then
                                        x = 1
                                        while true do
                                            a = _.Slots[y]
                                            d = packResults(tostring(x))
                                            a, i = a.FindFirstChild, a
                                            a = a(i, unpackResults(d))
                                            i = a
                                            if a then
                                                i = a:FindFirstChild("Data")
                                            end
                                            if i then
                                                i = a.Data:FindFirstChild("Player")
                                            end
                                            if i then
                                                if (a.Data.Player.Value == contextRef.value.lp) then
                                                    A = y
                                                    break
                                                end
                                                x = (x + 1)
                                                if (x > 4) then
                                                    break
                                                end
                                            else
                                                x = (x + 1)
                                                if (x > 4) then
                                                    break
                                                end
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                    v, y = f(s, z)
                                    z = v
                                    if (v == nil) then
                                        break
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                            if A then
                                f = (A == "Left")
                                if (A == "Left") then
                                    f = "Right"
                                end
                                if (not f) then
                                    f = "Left"
                                end
                                s = _.Slots:FindFirstChild(f)
                                if s then
                                    s = 1
                                    while true do
                                        y = _.Slots[f]
                                        b = packResults(tostring(s))
                                        y, x = y.FindFirstChild, y
                                        y = y(x, unpackResults(b))
                                        x = y
                                        if y then
                                            x = y:FindFirstChild("Data")
                                        end
                                        if x then
                                            x = y.Data:FindFirstChild("Player")
                                        end
                                        if x then
                                            b = y.Data.Player.Value
                                            if y.Data.Player.Value then
                                                b = (y.Data.Player.Value ~= contextRef.value.lp)
                                            end
                                            if b then
                                                table.insert(r, y.Data.Player.Value)
                                            end
                                        end
                                        s = (s + 1)
                                        if (s > 4) then
                                            break
                                        end
                                    end
                                    break
                                end
                                break
                            end
                            h, _ = m(p, k)
                            k = h
                            if (h == nil) then
                                break
                            end
                        else
                            h, _ = m(p, k)
                            k = h
                            if (h == nil) then
                                break
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return r
    end
end

callbacks.updateCameraAssist = function(valueRef, valueRef2, valueRef3, valueRef4, contextRef, valueRef5, valueRef6, valueRef7, valueRef8, valueRef9)
    return function(r)
        local A, D, _, a, f, h, k, m, p, v, x, y, z
        if (not contextRef.value.s.autoHoldActive) then
            return
        end
        m = contextRef.value.lp.Character
        if (not contextRef.value.lp.Character) then
            return
        end
        p = contextRef.value.lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        k = m:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        h = (not p)
        if p then
            h = (not k)
        end
        if (not h) then
            h = (k.Health <= 0)
        end
        if h then
            return
        end
        h = m:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
        h = {value = h}
        if (not h.value) then
            return
        end
        _ = valueRef8.value(h.value)
        if (not _) then
            _ = 99
        end
        if (_ <= valueRef9.value) then
            A = valueRef4.value()
            f = A
            if A then
                f = A.Character
            end
            if f then
                f = A.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if f then
                    v, z = p.Position, CFrame.new
                    y = packResults(Vector3.new(f.Position.X, p.Position.Y, f.Position.Z))
                    z = z(v, unpackResults(y))
                    z = p.CFrame:Lerp((z * CFrame.Angles(0, 2005.3522829578812, 0)), valueRef7.value)
                    p.CFrame = z
                    k:MoveTo(f.Position)
                    z, v = pcall, callbacks.invokeActivate(h)
                    z(v)
                end
            end
            return
        end
        A = valueRef4.value()
        f = (not A)
        if A then
            f = (not A.Character)
        end
        if f then
            return
        end
        f = A.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if (not f) then
            return
        end
        v = ((f.Position - valueRef.value)):Dot(p.CFrame.RightVector)
        if (v > 0.05) then
            valueRef6.value = -1
        else
            if (v < -0.05) then
                valueRef6.value = 1
            end
        end
        x, a, valueRef.value = valueRef3.value, valueRef2.value, f.Position
        valueRef3.value, D = (x + ((r * a) * valueRef6.value)), Vector3
        x = D.new
        D = math.sin(valueRef3.value)
        a = packResults(math.cos(valueRef3.value))
        x = x(D, 0, unpackResults(a))
        x = (f.Position + (x * valueRef5.value))
        p.CFrame = CFrame.new(p.Position, (p.Position + Vector3.new(((p.Position - f.Position)).Unit.X, 0, ((p.Position - f.Position)).Unit.Z)))
        k:MoveTo(x)
        return
    end
end

callbacks.stopShake = function(contextRef, connectionRef)
    return function()
        if (not contextRef.value.s.shakeActive) then
            return
        end
        contextRef.value.s.shakeActive = false
        if connectionRef.value then
            connectionRef.value:Disconnect()
            connectionRef.value = nil
        end
        contextRef.value.s.shakeTime = 0
        contextRef.value.s.shakeIndex = 1
        return
    end
end

callbacks.animateFlyingShard = function(valueRef)
    return function(r, m)
        local _, f, h, k, l, p, w
        p, k, h = ipairs(valueRef.value)
        p, k, h = normalizeIterator(p, k, h)
        _, l = p(k, h)
        h = _
        if (_ ~= nil) then
            while true do
                if l.p.Parent then
                    l.v = (l.v + Vector3.new(0, (-60 * m), 0))
                    f = l.p
                    w = (CFrame.new((l.p.Position + (l.v * m))) * (l.p.CFrame - l.p.Position))
                    f.CFrame = (w * CFrame.Angles((l.w.X * m), (l.w.Y * m), (l.w.Z * m)))
                    if (r > 0.6) then
                        l.p.Transparency = ((r - 0.6) / 0.4)
                    end
                end
                _, l = p(k, h)
                h = _
                if (_ == nil) then
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        return
    end
end

        h, _ = m(b, k)

            while true do
                f = tostring(_)
                if ((f .. "x") == selection) then
                    contextRef.value.s.shakeSpeedIndex = h
                    break
                end
                h, _ = m(b, k)
                k = h
                if (h == nil) then
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        return
    end
end

callbacks.attachAccessory = function(valueRef)
    return function(a, m)
        local _, f, h, i, k, l, n, p
        p = m:FindFirstChild("Handle")
        k = (not p)
        if p then
            k = (not p:IsA("BasePart"))
        end
        if k then
            m:Destroy()
            return
        end
        h, k = ipairs, nil
        _ = packResults(p:GetChildren())
        h, _, l = h(unpackResults(_))
        h, _, l = normalizeIterator(h, _, l)
        f, i = h(_, l)
        l = f
        if (f ~= nil) then
            while true do
                n = i:IsA("Attachment")
                if n then
                    k = i
                    break
                end
                f, i = h(_, l)
                l = f
                if (f == nil) then
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        if (not k) then
            m:Destroy()
            return
        end
        h, _ = valueRef.value(a, k.Name)
        l = h
        if h then
            l = _
        end
        if l then
            p.CFrame = ((h.CFrame * _.CFrame) * k.CFrame:Inverse())
            m.Parent = a
            p.CanCollide = false
            p.CanTouch = false
            p.CanQuery = false
            p.Massless = true
            l = Instance.new("WeldConstraint")
            l.Part0 = h
            l.Part1 = p
            l.Parent = p
            return
        end
        l = a:FindFirstChild("Head")
        h = l
        l = (not l)
        if l then
            m:Destroy()
            return
        end
        p.CFrame = (h.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0.5, 0))
        m.Parent = a
        p.CanCollide = false
        p.CanTouch = false
        p.CanQuery = false
        p.Massless = true
        l = Instance.new("WeldConstraint")
        l.Part0 = h
        l.Part1 = p
        l.Parent = p
        return
    end
end

    end

callbacks.findLowPopulationServer = function(valueRef, valueRef2, valueRef3, valueRef4, valueRef5)
    return function()
        local A, _, f, h, k, m, p, r, s, v, y, z
        p, r = {}, valueRef5.value
        p.Title = valueRef2.value("searchingServer")
        p.Content = "..."
        p.Duration = 5
        r, m = r.Notify, r
        r(m, p)
        r = {value = game.PlaceId}
        m = {value = ("https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/" .. (r.value .. "/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100"))}
        k, p = callbacks.invokeHttpGet(valueRef, m), pcall
        p, k = p(k)
        h = p
        if p then
            h = k
        end
        if h then
            h = k.data
        end
        if h then
            h = ((#k.data) > 0)
        end
        if h then
            h = nil
            _ = math.huge
            A, f, s = pairs(k.data)
            A, f, s = normalizeIterator(A, f, s)
            z, v = A(f, s)
            s = z
            if (z ~= nil) then
                while true do
                    y = v.id
                    if v.id then
                        y = v.playing
                    end
                    if y then
                        y = v.maxPlayers
                    end
                    if y then
                        y = (v.playing < v.maxPlayers)
                    end
                    if y then
                        y = (v.playing < _)
                    end
                    if y then
                        h, _ = v, v.playing
                    end
                    z, v = A(f, s)
                    s = z
                    if (z == nil) then
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
            if h then
                s, A = {}, valueRef5.value
                s.Title = valueRef2.value("serverFound")
                s.Content = "..."
                s.Duration = 3
                f, A = A, A.Notify
                A(f, s)
                wait(1)
                valueRef4.value:TeleportToPlaceInstance(r.value, h.id, valueRef3.value)
                return
            end
            h, A = valueRef5.value, {}
            A.Title = valueRef2.value("serverNotFound")
            A.Content = "..."
            A.Duration = 3
            h, _ = h.Notify, h
            h(_, A)
            wait(2)
            pcall(callbacks.teleportToSameServer(valueRef3, r, valueRef4))
            return
        end
        h, A = valueRef5.value, {}
        A.Title = valueRef2.value("serverNotFound")
        A.Content = "..."
        A.Duration = 3
        h, _ = h.Notify, h
        h(_, A)
        wait(2)
        pcall(callbacks.teleportToSameServer(valueRef3, r, valueRef4))
        return
    end

callbacks.animateFloatingModel = function(instanceRef, valueRef, instanceRef2, valueRef2, valueRef3)
    return function(r)
        local _, f, h, k, l, m, p, s
        m = math.min(1, (r / 0.06))
        p = (r < 0.06)
        if (r < 0.06) then
            p = 0
        end
        if (not p) then
            p = ((((r - 0.06) / 0.94) ^ 1.85) * 95)
        end
        k = Vector3.new((math.sin((r * 16)) * 1.3), p, 0)
        h = (math.sin((r * 8)) * 0.14)
        l, f, _ = valueRef3.value, ((valueRef.value + k) * CFrame.Angles(0, (r * 2.5), h)), valueRef3.value.PivotTo
        _(l, f)
        l = instanceRef.value
        instanceRef.value.Size = Vector3.new((0.5 + (6 * (1 - ((1 - m) ^ 3)))), ((0.5 + (6 * (1 - ((1 - m) ^ 3)))) * 1.15), (0.5 + (6 * (1 - ((1 - m) ^ 3)))))
        s = CFrame.new(((valueRef2.value + k) + Vector3.new(0, (6 + ((0.5 + (6 * (1 - ((1 - m) ^ 3)))) * 0.55)), 0)))
        l.CFrame = (s * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, h))
        instanceRef2.value.CFrame = CFrame.new(((valueRef2.value + k) + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0)))
        return
    end
end

callbacks.animateModelLift = function(valueRef, instanceRef, contextRef, valueRef2)
    return function()
        local a, b, e
        a = (not contextRef.value.s.speedActive)
        if contextRef.value.s.speedActive then
            a = (not instanceRef.value)
        end
        if (not a) then
            a = (not instanceRef.value.Parent)
        end
        if a then
            return
        end
        a = valueRef.value.MoveDirection
        if (valueRef.value.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0.01) then
            e = valueRef2.value
            b = packResults(math.clamp(contextRef.value.s.speedLevel, 1, 10))
            e = e(unpackResults(b))
            instanceRef.value.CFrame = (instanceRef.value.CFrame + (a * e))
        end
        return
    end
end

callbacks.invokeRing = function(valueRef, instanceRef)
    return function()
        valueRef.value.ring(instanceRef.value.Position, Color3.fromRGB(255, 90, 15), 28, 0.4)
        valueRef.value.shards(instanceRef.value.Position, Color3.fromRGB(50, 40, 35), Enum.Material.Slate, 16, 24, 1.2)
        return
    end
end

callbacks.clearCachedValue = function(contextRef, valueRef)
    return function()
        return contextRef.value.Players:GetUserIdFromNameAsync(valueRef.value)
    end
end

callbacks.setupSpeedCharacter = function(valueRef, valueRef2, valueRef3, valueRef4)
    return function(r, m)
        local _, b, e, f, g, h, k, l, p, s, t, v, w
        p, k = valueRef3.value()
        h, _ = m, nil
        l, f, s = ipairs(p)
        l, f, s = normalizeIterator(l, f, s)
        w, v = l(f, s)
        s = w
        if (w ~= nil) then
            while true do
                if v.Character then
                    g = v.Character
                    e = v.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    b = e
                    if e then
                        b = valueRef2.value(v)
                    end
                    if b then
                        b = valueRef.value(v, k)
                    end
                    if b then
                        b = ((e.Position - r.Position)).Magnitude
                        t = (((e.Position - r.Position)).Magnitude < h)
                        if (((e.Position - r.Position)).Magnitude < h) then
                            t = valueRef4.value(r, g, e)
                        end
                        if t then
                            h, _ = b, v
                        end
                    end
                end
                w, v = l(f, s)
                s = w
                if (w == nil) then
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        return _, h
    end
end

callbacks.applyModernKorblox = function(valueRef)
    return function(a)
        local _, h, k, l, m, p
        m = a:FindFirstChild("RightUpperLeg")
        if (not m) then
            return
        end
        m.Transparency = 1
        table.insert(valueRef.value, m)
        p = a:FindFirstChild("RightLowerLeg")
        k = a:FindFirstChild("RightFoot")
        if p then
            p.Transparency = 1
            table.insert(valueRef.value, p)
        end
        if k then
            k.Transparency = 1
            table.insert(valueRef.value, k)
        end
        h = Instance.new("Part")
        h.Size = Vector3.new(1, 2, 1)
        h.Anchored = false
        h.CanCollide = false
        h.Parent = a
        table.insert(valueRef.value, h)
        _ = Instance.new("SpecialMesh")
        _.MeshType = Enum.MeshType.FileMesh
        _.MeshId = "rbxassetid://101851696"
        _.TextureId = "rbxassetid://101851254"
        _.Parent = h
        table.insert(valueRef.value, _)
        l = Instance.new("Weld")
        l.Part0 = m
        l.Part1 = h
        l.C0 = CFrame.new(0, -0.8, 0)
        l.Parent = h
        table.insert(valueRef.value, l)
        return
    end
end

callbacks.playDeathEffectIfNearby = function(valueRef, valueRef2, valueRef3, contextRef, valueRef4)
    return function()
        local a, b, k, m
        if valueRef4.value then
            return
        end
        b, m, valueRef4.value = valueRef.value, valueRef2.value, true
        if m[b] then
            return
        end
        valueRef2.value[valueRef.value] = true
        if (contextRef.value.kfx.current <= 1) then
            return
        end
        a = contextRef.value.lp.Character
        if contextRef.value.lp.Character then
            a = contextRef.value.lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        end
        m = valueRef.value:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        k = a
        if a then
            k = m
        end
        if (not k) then
            return
        end
        if (((m.Position - a.Position)).Magnitude > contextRef.value.kfx.range) then
            return
        end
        valueRef3.value(contextRef.value.kfx.list[contextRef.value.kfx.current].id, valueRef.value)
        return
    end
end

callbacks.isPlayerAlive = function()
    return function(a)
        local e
        e = a
        if a then
            e = a.Character
        end
        if e then
            e = a.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
        end
        if e then
            return (a.Character.Humanoid.Health > 0)
        end
        return false
    end
end

callbacks.hasLineOfSight = function(contextRef)
    return function(r, m, p)
        local _, b, e, f, g, h, k, l, n, s, v
        k = RaycastParams.new()
        k.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
        _ = {}
        _[1] = contextRef.value.lp.Character
        k.FilterDescendantsInstances = _
        k.IgnoreWater = true
        h = (r.Position + Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0))
        f, l = p.Position, {}
        s = (p.Position + Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0))
        l[3], l[2], l[1] = (p.Position + Vector3.new(0, -1.5, 0)), s, f
        _ = l
        l = m:FindFirstChild("Head")
        if l then
            table.insert(_, l.Position)
        end
        f, s, n = ipairs(_)
        f, s, n = normalizeIterator(f, s, n)
        v, g = f(s, n)
        n = v
        if (v == nil) then
            return false
        end
        while true do
            e = workspace:Raycast(h, (g - h), k)
            b = e
            if e then
                b = e.Instance
            end
            if b then
                b = e.Instance:IsDescendantOf(m)
            end
            if b then
                return true
            end
            v, g = f(s, n)
            n = v
            if (v == nil) then
                return false
            end
        end
    end
end

callbacks.getPlayerDisplayName = function(valueRef)
    return function()
        local a, b, e, h, k
        a, e = pcall, callbacks.fetchDiscordInvite()
        a, e = a(e)
        if a then
            b = valueRef.value:JSONDecode(e)
            k = b.approximate_member_count
            if (not b.approximate_member_count) then
                k = "?"
            end
            h = b.approximate_presence_count
            if (not b.approximate_presence_count) then
                h = "?"
            end
            return k, h
        end
        return "?", "?"
    end
end

callbacks.invokeCreateHumanoidModelFromUserId = function(contextRef, valueRef)
    return function()
        return contextRef.value.Players:CreateHumanoidModelFromUserId(valueRef.value)
    end
end

callbacks.invokeFade4 = function(valueRef, valueRef2)
    return function()
        valueRef.value.fade(valueRef2.value, 0.8)
        return
    end
end

callbacks.hideCharacterForEffect = function(valueRef)
    return function(a)
        local b, e, g
        a = {value = a}
        e = {value = nil}
        e.value = callbacks.applyCharacterTransparency(a)
        e.value()
        b, g = task.spawn, callbacks.keepEffectCharacterHidden(valueRef, e, a)
        b(g)
        return
    end
end

callbacks.invokeWaitForChild = function(contextRef)
    return function(a)
        local e
        a:WaitForChild("Head")
        task.wait(0.2)
        e = a:FindFirstChild("Head")
        if e then
            if contextRef.value.s.headlessA then
                contextRef.value.fn.applyHeadless(e)
            else
                contextRef.value.fn.removeHeadless(e)
            end
        end
        return
    end
end

callbacks.toggleHeadless = function(contextRef)
    return function(enabled)
        local b, k
        b = contextRef.value
        contextRef.value.s.headlessA = enabled
        b = b.lp
        if b.Character then
            b, k = b.Character.FindFirstChild, b.Character
            b = b(k, "Head")
            if b then
                if enabled then
                    contextRef.value.fn.applyHeadless(b)
                else
                    contextRef.value.fn.removeHeadless(b)
                end
            end
        end
        return
    end
end

callbacks.prepareAvatarClone = function(valueRef, valueRef2, instanceRef)
    return function()
        local a, k, m
        a = instanceRef.value:IsA("Model")
        if a then
            valueRef2.value(instanceRef.value, "LevelOfDetail", "Disabled")
        else
            a = instanceRef.value:IsA("MeshPart")
            if a then
                a, m = valueRef2.value, instanceRef.value
                valueRef2.value(instanceRef.value, "CastShadow", false)
                a(m, "RenderFidelity", Enum.RenderFidelity.Performance)
            else
                a = instanceRef.value:IsA("BasePart")
                if a then
                    a, m = valueRef2.value, instanceRef.value
                    valueRef2.value(instanceRef.value, "CastShadow", false)
                    a(m, "Material", Enum.Material.Plastic)
                    a(m, "Reflectance", 0)
                else
                    a = instanceRef.value:IsA("Decal")
                    if (not a) then
                        a = instanceRef.value:IsA("Texture")
                    end
                    if a then
                        valueRef2.value(instanceRef.value, "Transparency", 1)
                    else
                        a = instanceRef.value:IsA("Fire")
                        if (not a) then
                            a = instanceRef.value:IsA("Smoke")
                        end
                        if (not a) then
                            a = instanceRef.value:IsA("Sparkles")
                        end
                        if (not a) then
                            a = instanceRef.value:IsA("SpotLight")
                        end
                        if a then
                            valueRef2.value(instanceRef.value, "Enabled", false)
                        else
                            a = instanceRef.value:IsA("ParticleEmitter")
                            if (not a) then
                                a = instanceRef.value:IsA("Trail")
                            end
                            if (not a) then
                                a = instanceRef.value:IsA("Beam")
                            end
                            if a then
                                valueRef2.value(instanceRef.value, "Enabled", false)
                            else
                                a = instanceRef.value:IsA("Sky")
                                if a then
                                    if (not valueRef.value[instanceRef.value]) then
                                        k = instanceRef.value
                                        valueRef.value[instanceRef.value] = instanceRef.value.Parent
                                        k.Parent = nil
                                    end
                                else
                                    a = instanceRef.value:IsA("Atmosphere")
                                    if a then
                                        m, a = instanceRef.value, valueRef2.value
                                        valueRef2.value(instanceRef.value, "Density", 0)
                                        a(m, "Haze", 0)
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return
    end
end

callbacks.updateAutoHoldPosition = function(contextRef)
    return function(r, m, p)
        local _, f, k, l, n
        if (contextRef.value.UIS.MouseBehavior == Enum.MouseBehavior.LockCenter) then
            return
        end
        k = Vector3.new(m.X, r.Position.Y, m.Z)
        if (((k - r.Position)).Magnitude < 0.5) then
            return
        end
        _ = CFrame.lookAt(r.Position, k)
        l = r.CFrame
        n = packResults(math.clamp((p * 10), 0, 1))
        f, l = l, l.Lerp
        l = l(f, _, unpackResults(n))
        r.CFrame = l
        return
    end
end

callbacks.updateTransparency = function(valueRef, valueRef2, valueRef3)
    return function(r)
        local _, f, g, h, i, k, l, m, o
        m = (math.sin((r * 9)) * 0.85)
        k, l = valueRef3.value, valueRef2.value
        f = CFrame.new
        o = math.cos((r * 6))
        o = (o * 0.5)
        f = f(m, (15 * r), o)
        g = math
        _, h, k = ((l * f) * CFrame.Angles(0, ((r * math.pi) * 3), 0)), k, k.PivotTo
        k(h, _)
        k = (0.3 + (0.7 * g.max(0, ((r - 0.5) / 0.5))))
        h, _, l = ipairs(valueRef.value)
        h, _, l = normalizeIterator(h, _, l)
        f, i = h(_, l)
        l = f
        if (f ~= nil) then
            while true do
                if (i.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart") then
                    i.Transparency = k
                end
                f, i = h(_, l)
                l = f
                if (f == nil) then
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        return
    end
end

callbacks.storeUsernameInput = function(valueRef, valueRef2)
    return function()

        a = a(e, "%s+$", "")
        a = {value = a}
        if (a.value ~= "") then
            b, e = callbacks.invokeSkinApply(valueRef2, a), task.spawn
            e(b)
        end
        return
    end
end

callbacks.setGuiVisibility = function(valueRef, valueRef2, instanceRef)
    return function()
        local _, a, h, k, m
        a = instanceRef.value:IsA("GuiObject")
        if a then
            if valueRef2.value then
                if (valueRef.value[instanceRef.value] == nil) then
                    k, a, m = {}, valueRef.value, instanceRef.value
                    _ = instanceRef.value
                    k.bg = instanceRef.value.BackgroundTransparency
                    _, h = _, _.IsA
                    h = h(_, "TextLabel")
                    if (not h) then
                        h = instanceRef.value:IsA("TextButton")
                    end
                    if (not h) then
                        h = instanceRef.value:IsA("TextBox")
                    end
                    if h then
                        h = instanceRef.value.TextTransparency
                    end
                    if (not h) then
                        h = nil
                    end
                    k.txt = h
                    h = instanceRef.value:IsA("ImageLabel")
                    if (not h) then
                        h = instanceRef.value:IsA("ImageButton")
                    end
                    if h then
                        h = instanceRef.value.ImageTransparency
                    end
                    if (not h) then
                        h = nil
                    end
                    k.img = h
                    a[m] = k
                end
                a = instanceRef.value
                instanceRef.value.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                m, a = a, a.IsA
                a = a(m, "TextLabel")
                if (not a) then
                    a = instanceRef.value:IsA("TextButton")
                end
                if (not a) then
                    a = instanceRef.value:IsA("TextBox")
                end
                if a then
                    instanceRef.value.TextTransparency = 1
                end
                a = instanceRef.value:IsA("ImageLabel")
                if (not a) then
                    a = instanceRef.value:IsA("ImageButton")
                end
                if a then
                    instanceRef.value.ImageTransparency = 1
                end
            else
                a = valueRef.value[instanceRef.value]
                if valueRef.value[instanceRef.value] then
                    instanceRef.value.BackgroundTransparency = valueRef.value[instanceRef.value].bg
                    if a.txt then
                        instanceRef.value.TextTransparency = a.txt
                    end
                    if a.img then
                        instanceRef.value.ImageTransparency = a.img
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return
    end
end

return callbacks.bootstrap()(...)
local callbacks = {}

local function packResults(...)
    return { {...}, select("#", ...) }
end
local unpackValues = table.unpack or unpack
local function unpackResults(values)
    return unpackValues(values[1], 1, values[2])
end
local function normalizeIterator(iterator, state, key)
    if type(iterator) ~= "function" then
        local mt = getmetatable(iterator)
        if mt and mt.__iter then
            return mt.__iter(iterator)
        elseif (not mt or not mt.__call) and type(iterator) == "table" then
            return pairs(iterator)
        end
    end
    return iterator, state, key
end


callbacks.effectLightning = function(valueRef, valueRef2, valueRef3, valueRef4)
    return function(r, m)
        local _, e, f, h, i, k, l, n, o, p
        r = {value = r}
        valueRef3.value.strip(r.value)
        p = valueRef3.value.parts(r.value)
        p = {value = p}
        k = {value = m.Position}
        h = Instance.new("PointLight", m)
        h = {value = h}
        h.value.Color = Color3.fromRGB(200, 150, 255)
        h.value.Range = 55
        h.value.Brightness = 0
        _, i = ipairs, {}
        i[3], i[2], i[1] = 0.22, 0.09, 0
        _, l, f = _(i)
        _, l, f = normalizeIterator(_, l, f)
        i, n = _(l, f)
        f = i
        if (i ~= nil) then
            while true do
                o, e = task.delay, callbacks.runLightningPulse(p, k, valueRef2, h, valueRef3, valueRef)
                o(n, e)
                i, n = _(l, f)
                f = i
                if (i == nil) then
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        valueRef3.value.ring(k.value, Color3.fromRGB(160, 100, 255), 28, 0.4)
        valueRef4.value(k.value, "rbxasset://sounds/impact_explosion_03.mp3", 3, 1.3)
        valueRef3.value.shards(k.value, Color3.fromRGB(220, 180, 255), Enum.Material.Neon, 16, 28, 0.6)
        f, _ = callbacks.invokeFade(valueRef3, r), task.delay
        _(2.8, f)
        return
    end
end

callbacks.applyUsernameAppearance = function(valueRef, valueRef2, valueRef3, valueRef4, valueRef5, contextRef, valueRef6)
    return function(r, m)
        local _, b, e, f, g, h, k, l, n, o, p, s
        p = valueRef5.value
        if (not valueRef5.value) then
            p = (not r)
        end
        if (not p) then
            p = (r == "")
        end
        if p then
            return false
        end
        valueRef5.value, p = true, valueRef2.value
        p = p(r)
        p = {value = p}
        if (not p.value) then
            valueRef5.value = false
            return false
        end
        k = {value = contextRef.value.lp.Character}
        if (not k.value) then
            valueRef5.value = false
            return false
        end
        _, h = callbacks.invokeCreateHumanoidModelFromUserId(contextRef, p), pcall
        h, _ = h(_)
        l = (not h)
        if h then
            l = (not _)
        end
        if l then
            valueRef5.value = false
            return false
        end
        valueRef4.value(k.value)
        l = ipairs
        f = packResults(_:GetChildren())
        l, f, s = l(unpackResults(f))
        l, f, s = normalizeIterator(l, f, s)
        n, o = l(f, s)
        s = n
        if (n ~= nil) then
            while true do
                g = o:IsA("Shirt")
                if (not g) then
                    g = o:IsA("Pants")
                end
                if (not g) then
                    g = o:IsA("ShirtGraphic")
                end
                if (not g) then
                    g = o:IsA("BodyColors")
                end
                if (not g) then
                    g = o:IsA("CharacterMesh")
                end
                if (not g) then
                    g = o:IsA("TShirt")
                end
                if g then
                    o:Clone().Parent = k.value
                end
                n, o = l(f, s)
                s = n
                if (n == nil) then
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        valueRef3.value(k.value, p.value)
        l = ipairs
        f = packResults(_:GetChildren())
        l, f, s = l(unpackResults(f))
        l, f, s = normalizeIterator(l, f, s)
        n, o = l(f, s)
        s = n
        if (n ~= nil) then
            while true do
                g = o:IsA("Accessory")
                if g then
                    g = o:Clone()
                    g = {value = g}
                    b, e = callbacks.attachClonedAccessory(g, k, valueRef), pcall
                    e(b)
                end
                n, o = l(f, s)
                s = n
                if (n == nil) then
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        _:Destroy()
        valueRef6.value = r
        valueRef5.value = false
        return true
    end
end

callbacks.applyLighting = function(valueRef, valueRef2, contextRef)
    return function(enabled)
        local _, b, h, k, l, m
        contextRef.value.s.lightingActive = enabled
        if enabled then
            contextRef.value.Lighting.Brightness = 2
            contextRef.value.Lighting.ColorShift_Bottom = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
            contextRef.value.Lighting.ColorShift_Top = Color3.fromRGB(255, 245, 230)
            contextRef.value.Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(128, 128, 128)
            contextRef.value.Lighting.GeographicLatitude = 45
            m, b, k = pairs(valueRef2.value)
            m, b, k = normalizeIterator(m, b, k)
            h, _ = m(b, k)
            k = h
            if (h ~= nil) then
                while true do
                    l = _
                    if _ then
                        l = _.Parent
                    end
                    if l then
                        _:Destroy()
                    end
                    h, _ = m(b, k)
                    k = h
                    if (h == nil) then
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
            b = {}
            b, valueRef2.value = Instance, b
            m = b.new("BloomEffect", contextRef.value.Lighting)
            m.Name = "PX_Bloom"
            m.Intensity = 0.5
            m.Size = 24
            m.Threshold = 0.9
            table.insert(valueRef2.value, m)
            b = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect", contextRef.value.Lighting)
            b.Name = "PX_CC"
            b.Brightness = 0.05
            b.Contrast = 0.1
            b.Saturation = 0.15
            table.insert(valueRef2.value, b)
            k = Instance.new("SunRaysEffect", contextRef.value.Lighting)
            k.Name = "PX_SunRays"
            k.Intensity = 0.1
            k.Spread = 1
            table.insert(valueRef2.value, k)
        else
            k = valueRef.value
            contextRef.value.Lighting.Brightness = valueRef.value.Brightness
            contextRef.value.Lighting.ColorShift_Bottom = k.ColorShift_Bottom
            contextRef.value.Lighting.ColorShift_Top = k.ColorShift_Top
            contextRef.value.Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = k.OutdoorAmbient
            contextRef.value.Lighting.GeographicLatitude = k.GeographicLatitude
            m, b, k = pairs(valueRef2.value)
            m, b, k = normalizeIterator(m, b, k)
            h, _ = m(b, k)
            k = h
            if (h ~= nil) then
                while true do
                    l = _
                    if _ then
                        l = _.Parent
                    end
                    if l then
                        _:Destroy()
                    end
                    h, _ = m(b, k)
                    k = h
                    if (h == nil) then
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
            b = {}
            valueRef2.value = b
        end
        return
    end
end

callbacks.setRecording = function(contextRef, valueRef, valueRef2)
    return function(r)
        local _, f, g, h, k, l, n, o, p, s
        p = pairs
        k = packResults(game.CoreGui:GetChildren())
        p, k, h = p(unpackResults(k))
        p, k, h = normalizeIterator(p, k, h)
        _, l = p(k, h)
        h = _
        if (_ ~= nil) then
            while true do
                f = l:IsA("ScreenGui")
                if f then
                    f = l.Name:find("Phantom")
                    if (not f) then
                        f = l.Name:find("Wind")
                    end
                    if (not f) then
                        f = l.Name:find("Dance")
                    end
                    if (not f) then
                        f = l.Name:find("InvisibleGhost")
                    end
                end
                if f then
                    f = ipairs
                    s = packResults(l:GetDescendants())
                    f, s, n = f(unpackResults(s))
                    f, s, n = normalizeIterator(f, s, n)
                    o, g = f(s, n)
                    n = o
                    if (o ~= nil) then
                        while true do
                            valueRef2.value(g, r)
                            o, g = f(s, n)
                            n = o
                            if (o == nil) then
                                break
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                _, l = p(k, h)
                h = _
                if (_ == nil) then
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        if (not r) then
            k = {}
            valueRef.value = k
        end
        if contextRef.value.fn.updateFloatingToggleVisibility then
            contextRef.value.fn.updateFloatingToggleVisibility()
        end
        if contextRef.value.fn.updateAssistFloatingVisual then
            contextRef.value.fn.updateAssistFloatingVisual()
        end
        return
    end
end

callbacks.animateGhostModel = function(instanceRef, valueRef, valueRef2, valueRef3)
    return function()
        local _, h, l, m, p, r
        r = tick()
        while true do
            m = (tick() - r)
            if (m < valueRef2.value) then
                m, p, h = ((1 - ((tick() - r) / valueRef2.value)) * valueRef.value), instanceRef.value, valueRef3.value
                _ = CFrame.new
                l = ((math.random() - 0.5) * m)
                _ = _(l, ((math.random() - 0.5) * m), 0)
                p.CFrame = (h * _)
                task.wait(0.02)
            else
                break
            end
        end
        instanceRef.value.CFrame = valueRef3.value
        return
    end
end

callbacks.runLightningPulse = function(valueRef, valueRef2, contextRef, valueRef3, valueRef4, valueRef5)
    return function()
        local _, a, h, k, l, m, p
        a, p = valueRef4.value.bolt, valueRef2.value
        k = Vector3.new
        h = valueRef5.value:NextNumber(-6, 6)
        l = packResults(valueRef5.value:NextNumber(-6, 6))
        k = k(h, 95, unpackResults(l))
        a((p + k), p, Color3.fromRGB(180, 120, 255), 0.45)
        valueRef3.value.Brightness = 18
        p, a = valueRef3.value, contextRef.value.TS
        k = TweenInfo.new(0.3)
        h = {}
        h.Brightness = 0
        a, m = a.Create, a
        a = a(m, p, k, h)
        m, a = a, a.Play
        a(m)
        valueRef4.value.paint(valueRef.value, Color3.fromRGB(240, 230, 255), Enum.Material.Neon, 0)
        a = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect", contextRef.value.Lighting)
        a.Brightness = 0.4
        a.Saturation = -0.4
        m = contextRef.value.TS
        h = TweenInfo.new(0.35)
        _ = {}
        _.Brightness = 0
        _.Saturation = 0
        p, m = m, m.Create
        m = m(p, a, h, _)
        m, p = m.Play, m
        m(p)
        contextRef.value.Debris:AddItem(a, 0.5)
        return
    end
end

callbacks.updateFloatingToggleVisibility = function(instanceRef, contextRef)
    return function()
        local a, e
        a = (not instanceRef.value)
        if instanceRef.value then
            a = (not instanceRef.value.Parent)
        end
        if a then
            return
        end
        a = contextRef.value.s
        if contextRef.value.s then
            a = contextRef.value.s.recActive
        end
        if a then
            a = instanceRef.value
            instanceRef.value.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            a.TextTransparency = 1
            e, a = a, a.FindFirstChildOfClass
            a = a(e, "UIStroke")
            if a then
                instanceRef.value:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke").Transparency = 1
            end
            a = instanceRef.value:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGradient")
            if a then
                instanceRef.value:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGradient").Transparency = NumberSequence.new(1)
            end
        else
            a = instanceRef.value
            instanceRef.value.BackgroundTransparency = 0
            a.TextTransparency = 0
            a, e = a.FindFirstChildOfClass, a
            a = a(e, "UIStroke")
            if a then
                instanceRef.value:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke").Transparency = 0
            end
            a = instanceRef.value:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGradient")
            if a then
                instanceRef.value:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGradient").Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0)
            end
        end
        return
    end
end

callbacks.invokeClock = function(connectionRef, valueRef, valueRef2, valueRef3)
    return function(a)
        local e
        e = ((os.clock() - valueRef2.value) / valueRef.value)
        if (e >= 1) then
            connectionRef.value:Disconnect()
            pcall(valueRef3.value, 1, a)
            return
        end
        pcall(valueRef3.value, e, a)
        return
    end
end

callbacks.effectDarkHeart = function(valueRef, valueRef2, valueRef3)
    return function(a, m)
        local f, h, i, k, l, n, o, p
        a = {value = a}
        valueRef3.value.strip(a.value)
        p = valueRef3.value.parts(a.value)
        valueRef3.value.paint(p, Color3.fromRGB(10, 8, 14), Enum.Material.Slate, 0, 0.7)
        k = {value = m.Position}
        h, l = task.delay, callbacks.fadeDestroyedModel(k, a, valueRef3)
        h(1, l)
        h = 1
        f = 1
        while true do
            f = {value = f}
            i = task.delay
            n, o = (0.13 * f.value), callbacks.createHeartBillboard(f, valueRef2, valueRef, valueRef3, k)
            i(n, o)
            h = (h + 1)
            f = h
            if (h > 8) then
                break
            end
        end
        return
    end
end

        end

    end

callbacks.findArenaOpponents = function(valueRef, contextRef)
    return function()
        local A, B, D, _, a, f, h, k, m, p, r, s, v, x, z
        r = tick()
        if ((r - valueRef.value.time) < 0.1) then
            return valueRef.value.enemies, valueRef.value.arena, valueRef.value.mySide
        end
        p = {}
        p, m, k = nil, p, nil
        h = workspace:FindFirstChild("Arenas")
        if h then
            h = pairs
            _ = packResults(workspace.Arenas:GetChildren())
            h, _, A = h(unpackResults(_))
            h, _, A = normalizeIterator(h, _, A)
            f, s = h(_, A)
            A = f
            if (f ~= nil) then
                while true do
                    z = (f == "Arena5")
                    if (f ~= "Arena5") then
                        z = (f == "Arena5ICED")
                    end
                    if z then
                        f, s = h(_, A)
                        A = f
                        if (f == nil) then
                            break
                        end
                    else
                        z = s:FindFirstChild("Slots")
                        if z then
                            z = s.Slots:FindFirstChild("Left")
                            if z then
                                z = 1
                                while true do
                                    x = s.Slots.Left
                                    B = packResults(tostring(z))
                                    x, D = x.FindFirstChild, x
                                    x = x(D, unpackResults(B))
                                    D = x
                                    if x then
                                        D = x:FindFirstChild("Data")
                                    end
                                    if D then
                                        D = x.Data:FindFirstChild("Player")
                                    end
                                    if D then
                                        if (x.Data.Player.Value == contextRef.value.lp) then
                                            p, k = "Left", s
                                            break
                                        end
                                        z = (z + 1)
                                        if (z > 4) then
                                            break
                                        end
                                    else
                                        z = (z + 1)
                                        if (z > 4) then
                                            break
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                            z = (not k)
                            if (not k) then
                                z = s.Slots:FindFirstChild("Right")
                            end
                            if z then
                                z = 1
                                while true do
                                    x = s.Slots.Right
                                    B = packResults(tostring(z))
                                    x, D = x.FindFirstChild, x
                                    x = x(D, unpackResults(B))
                                    D = x
                                    if x then
                                        D = x:FindFirstChild("Data")
                                    end
                                    if D then
                                        D = x.Data:FindFirstChild("Player")
                                    end
                                    if D then
                                        if (x.Data.Player.Value == contextRef.value.lp) then
                                            k, p = s, "Right"
                                            break
                                        end
                                        z = (z + 1)
                                        if (z > 4) then
                                            break
                                        end
                                    else
                                        z = (z + 1)
                                        if (z > 4) then
                                            break
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                            z = p
                            if p then
                                z = k
                            end
                            if z then
                                z = (p == "Left")
                                if (p == "Left") then
                                    z = "Right"
                                end
                                if (not z) then
                                    z = "Left"
                                end
                                v = k.Slots:FindFirstChild(z)
                                if v then
                                    v = 1
                                    while true do
                                        D = k.Slots[z]
                                        a = packResults(tostring(v))
                                        B, D = D, D.FindFirstChild
                                        D = D(B, unpackResults(a))
                                        B = D
                                        if D then
                                            B = D:FindFirstChild("Data")
                                        end
                                        if B then
                                            B = D.Data:FindFirstChild("Player")
                                        end
                                        if B then
                                            a = D.Data.Player.Value
                                            if D.Data.Player.Value then
                                                a = (D.Data.Player.Value ~= contextRef.value.lp)
                                            end
                                            if a then
                                                table.insert(m, D.Data.Player.Value)
                                            end
                                        end
                                        v = (v + 1)
                                        if (v > 4) then
                                            break
                                        end
                                    end
                                    break
                                end
                                break
                            end
                            f, s = h(_, A)
                            A = f
                            if (f == nil) then
                                break
                            end
                        else
                            f, s = h(_, A)
                            A = f
                            if (f == nil) then
                                break
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        _ = valueRef.value
        valueRef.value.enemies, f, A = m, valueRef.value, valueRef.value
        _.arena = k
        A.mySide = p
        f.time = r
        return m, k, p
    end
end

callbacks.prepareBombGrabCharacter = function(valueRef, contextRef, connectionRef)
    return function(a)
        local _, b, e, h, k
        e = a:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
        e = {value = e}
        b = a:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
        b = {value = b}
        k = (not e.value)
        if e.value then
            k = (not b.value)
        end
        if k then
            return
        end
        if connectionRef.value then
            connectionRef.value:Disconnect()
        end
        _, k = callbacks.updateVelocity(e, b, contextRef, valueRef), contextRef.value.RunService.RenderStepped
        k, h = k.Connect, k
        k = k(h, _)
        connectionRef.value = k
        return
    end
end

        h = h(_, g)
        k.value = h
        return k.value
    end
end
